**[00:04:13.280] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000043)  
and its attached agencies and corporations,

**[00:04:16.440] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000044)  
our resource persons, distinguished guests,

**[00:04:20.020] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000045)  
Maayong hapon sa aton tanan.

**[00:04:23.940] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000046)  
Today's budget briefing gives the committee an opportunity

**[00:04:26.600] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000047)  
to assess the department's performance

**[00:04:30.100] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000048)  
under the fiscal year 2026 budget, review the implementation

**[00:04:37.980] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000049)  
of its major programs, and determine whether the proposal

**[00:04:43.000] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000050)  
or the proposed fiscal year 2027 budget is adequate

**[00:04:45.800] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000051)  
and responsive to the country's continuing housing needs.

**[00:04:48.840] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000052)  
We recognize the progress achieved under the expanded

**[00:04:53.400] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000053)  
Pabansang-Pabahay para sa Pilipino, or 4PH program,

**[00:04:57.780] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000054)  
particularly the adoption

**[00:05:00.800] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000055)  
of more flexible housing modalities and the mobilization of private sector participation.

**[00:05:09.120] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000056)  
Stronger coordination with LGUs and other national government agencies is likewise essential

**[00:05:15.400] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000057)  
to translating these initiatives into affordable and sustainable housing options for Filipino

**[00:05:22.900] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000058)  
families.

**[00:05:24.140] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000059)  
At the same time, serious challenges remain.

**[00:05:29.200] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000060)  
These include the substantial housing deficit, limited access to suitable land and affordable

**[00:05:37.900] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000061)  
housing or financing, permitting and implementation delays, and the need to ensure the approved

**[00:05:45.600] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000062)  
projects are funded, completed, and turned over to qualified beneficiaries.

**[00:05:52.080] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000063)  
The committee is particularly concerned about the persistent gap between the national housing

**[00:05:58.400] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000064)  
requirements and the department's current delivery targets.

**[00:06:04.000] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000065)  
For fiscal year 2027, the proposed budget must provide clear and concrete responses

**[00:06:11.300] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000066)  
to these challenges.

**[00:06:14.180] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000067)  
Under the fiscal year 2027 NEP, DHSUD and its attached agencies and corporations are allocated a combined ₱5.498 billion.

**[00:06:28.820] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000068)  
And this ₱579.55 million, or about 9.5%, below ₱6.78 billion provided under the 2026 fiscal year GAA.

**[00:06:47.280] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000069)  
And ₱54.52 billion, or ₱5.2 billion lower than the agency's aggregate ₱60 billion budget proposal. Of the net amount, ₱2.492 billion is allocated to DHSUD.

**[00:07:05.280] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000101)  
₱2.274 billion to NHA, ₱566 million to HSAC, ₱1.66 million to SHFC, and ₱450,000 to NHFC.

**[00:07:25.260] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000102)  
Given these differences, the Committee will closely examine fiscal year 2026 fund utilization and accomplishment, the implementation status of ongoing 4PH projects, the causes of delays or underspending, and the measures proposed to accelerate housing delivery.

**[00:07:49.060] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000103)  
Above all, every peso appropriated for housing must translate

**[00:07:55.400] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000104)  
into measurable results, more completed and occupied units, broader access to affordable housing, and safer and more dignified living conditions for Filipino families.

**[00:08:11.420] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000105)  
We look forward once again to focus on constructive discussion with DHSUD and its attached agencies and corporations.

**[00:08:21.740] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000106)  
And with this, we now invite the department to proceed with its presentation.

**[00:08:26.700] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000107)  
Maraming salamat sa ating lahat.

**[00:08:33.267] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000108)  
Secretary Aliling, you can...

**[00:08:35.056] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000110)  
Maraming salamat po, Chairperson Jam Baronda, former President Gloria Macapagal-Arroyo, members of this committee, Chairperson Marie V. Copilar of the Housing Committee.

**[00:08:47.480] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000111)  
Lahat po nung nandito ngayon, magandang umaga.

**[00:08:51.240] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000112)  
Nandito po kami ngayon para i-present sa inyo, unang-una ano ba yung mga ating mga na-accomplish itong nakaraan taon

**[00:08:57.400] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000113)  
at ano yung mga

**[00:08:59.240] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000114)  
kakailanganin po natin sa susunod na taon.

**[00:09:04.000] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000115)  
Pagdating po sa pag-address ng housing need na based po sa PSA

**[00:09:08.360] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000116)  
ay 3.7 million, 3.7 million po, hindi po 6.9 million hanggang 2028.

**[00:09:16.300] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000117)  
Isa po sa mga highlights ng aming presentation ay mag-aapila po kami

**[00:09:23.015] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000118)  
na paano po natin paparamihin yung iba nating mga programa sa pabahay.

**[00:09:29.400] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000119)  
Dahil po ang 3.7 million na housing needs by 2028, hindi po ibig sabihin yan kailangan natin gumawa ng 3.7 million na bahay.

**[00:09:38.960] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000120)  
Dahil ang 3.7 million po na yan na sinasabi ng PSA na magiging housing needs natin by 2028, nakahati po yan sa tatlong kategories.

**[00:09:49.040] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000121)  
Number one, ito po, meron po mga kasalukuyang may bahay ngayon, ngunit kailangan na ayusin.

**[00:09:57.940] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000122)  
So ang kailangan po niyan, hindi po bahay at lupa na bago.

**[00:10:01.040] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000123)  
Ang kailangan po niyan, unang-una,

**[00:10:02.760] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000124)  
mapag-andar natin na mas mabilis ang community mortgage program.

**[00:10:06.820] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000125)  
At pag paandar po natin ang community mortgage program,

**[00:10:10.840] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000126)  
in-expand po natin yung CMP

**[00:10:12.880] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000127)  
para mapaayos po nila

**[00:10:14.440] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000128)  
ang kanilang mga tahanan.

**[00:10:16.460] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000129)  
So yung 1.1 million po na yan,

**[00:10:19.800] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000130)  
ang solusyon dyan CMP and then yung in-expand po natin ang programa ng SHFC

**[00:10:25.620] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000131)  
pati na po yung pampagawa ng bahay

**[00:10:27.440] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000132)  
at utilities nila.

**[00:10:28.440] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000133)  
Kunyari binabaha, kailangan na mga ayusin yung mga site development, kasama na po dyan.

**[00:10:34.440] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000134)  
Then, nandyan din po sa solusyon sa number one category na yan

**[00:10:37.950] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000135)  
ay ang home improvement loan ng Pag-IBIG Fund.

**[00:10:42.060] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000136)  
Pangalawang kategorya po, ito po ang tinatawag nating mga double up.

**[00:10:45.860] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000137)  
Ano po ba itong double up?

**[00:10:47.480] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000138)  
Ang double up po, ito po yung may mga bahay na mas marami sa isang pamilya ang nakatira.

**[00:10:54.600] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000139)  
So, ang hinahighlight lang po natin sa pangalawang kategorya na ito,

**[00:10:58.100] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000140)  
hindi po ito nalilimit sa informal settler families.

**[00:11:03.140] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000141)  
Lahat po, yung buong spektrum po ng housing, sinasabi ko nga para maintindihan,

**[00:11:11.000] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000142)  
mula sa ISF areas hanggang sa Forbes Park ang nakatira,

**[00:11:11.880] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000143)  
kung more than one family po ang nakatira sa bahay na yun, kasama po dito sa numero na ito.

**[00:11:19.160] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000144)  
Pangatlo, 1.1 million po ang projected ng PSA na magkakaroon ng sariling pamilya.

**[00:11:26.600] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000145)  
Ito po,

**[00:11:27.000] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000146)  
ang kailangan nito kung mag-dedecision silang humiwalay sa kanilang mga present na pamilya, sa kanilang mga magulang,

**[00:11:36.000] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000147)  
makangilangan po ito ng bahay.

**[00:11:39.880] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000148)  
Pangatlo, dito po nagkaroon ng diferensya yung 6.9 million sa 3.7 million na housing needs.

**[00:11:47.760] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000149)  
Doon po sa 6.9 million, yung unang pong study,

**[00:11:50.860] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000150)  
Ang assumption po doon, yung inventory loss, ito yung may mga bahay ngayon na masisira

**[00:11:56.620] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000151)  
through wear and tear, bagyo, earthquake, etc.

**[00:11:59.600] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000152)  
Nakalagay po doon, 3.7 million ang projected na loss in inventory.

**[00:12:06.400] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000153)  
Dito po, sa 3.7 million na housing need na sinasabi ng PSA,

**[00:12:11.600] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000154)  
ang sinasabi lang dito, kung meron pong masisirang 3.7 million, meron pong magpapagawa.

**[00:12:18.920] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000155)  
So pag binawas nyo po yung magpapagawa, ang sinasabi po ng latest na PSA na survey o study,

**[00:12:25.140] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000156)  
ang magiging loss ng inventory lang is P399,000.

**[00:12:30.620] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000157)  
So yun lang po ang naging diferensya nung P6.9 million na unang sinasabi nating projected na housing needs by 2028

**[00:12:40.040] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000158)  
versus itong P3.7 million na latest na linabas po ng PSA.

**[00:12:45.320] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000159)  
So ito po, sinasabi lang namin, ang 4PH alone, hindi siya ang solusyon dito sa housing needs na 3.7.

**[00:12:54.100] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000160)  
Kasi tulad niya po na nasabi ko, kasama po dito yung formal communities.

**[00:12:59.900] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000161)  
So pati po yung gagawin ng mga bahay, ng mga private developers,

**[00:13:07.380] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000162)  
ito po kasama po ito sa magsasolve ng 3.7 million na housing backlog natin by 2028.

**[00:13:14.940] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000163)  
Pagdating naman po sa regulatory, tayo po ay binabalansin natin ang interest ng mga developers at ng mga homebuyers.

**[00:13:23.400] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000164)  
So kung ito po mga nakarang araw, maririnig po natin o mababasa natin sa mga social media

**[00:13:30.900] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000165)  
na may mga umapila sa amin ang mga developers na nakahihirapan po kumuha ng mga license to sell.

**[00:13:37.180] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000166)  
So inayos po natin ito, basically 10 weeks na po

**[00:13:41.360] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000167)  
Ten weeks na po, consecutive weeks, ang DHS-UD na zero pending overdue sa regulatory applications.

**[00:13:49.700] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000168)  
Ang ibig pong sabihin nito, lahat po ng regulatory applications nitong nakaraang sampung linggo ay naaksyonan within 15 days.

**[00:14:01.580] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000169)  
within 15 days, 1-5, 15 days.

**[00:14:03.780] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000170)  
Ano po ba itong mga aksyon na ito?

**[00:14:07.580] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000171)  
Una-una, pwedeng ma-approve ang kanilang mga application.

**[00:14:09.840] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000172)  
Pangalawa, pwedeng ma-deny.

**[00:14:14.100] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000173)  
Pangatlo po, pwede po silang ma-issuehan ng Notice of Deficiencies.

**[00:14:22.680] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000174)  
Pag sila po ay na-issuehan ng Notice of Deficiencies, meron po silang 90 days to comply.

**[00:14:26.980] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000175)  
Ngayon, ang ginawa po natin para madalian po silang mag-comply,

**[00:14:30.500] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000176)  
naglabas po tayo ng guidelines na bago na

**[00:14:34.620] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000177)  
they are allowed to apply for temporary license to sell

**[00:14:38.980] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000178)  
habang kinukumply po nila ang kanilang mga deficiencies.

**[00:14:41.440] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000179)  
So nito pong nakaraang dalawang buwan,

**[00:14:46.280] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000180)  
ito pong July and towards the end of June, July and August,

**[00:14:56.000] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000181)  
nakita po natin ang numero po na qualify or na-approve na mga license to sell ay tumaas po.

**[00:15:00.000] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000182)  
In fact, yung numero po ng ating mga license to sell

**[00:15:05.460] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000183)  
ating mga license to sell na na-approve or na-qualify for any kind of license to sell.

**[00:15:13.440] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000184)  
Pwede po siyang regular license to sell, conditional license to sell, or temporary license to sell.

**[00:15:25.940] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000185)  
Makikita po natin, on the same period po ng last year, 2025, pareho na po halos ang ating numero ng 2025 and 2026.

**[00:15:29.500] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000186)  
Pagdating naman po sa mga reklamo ng mga homebuyers,

**[00:15:35.860] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000187)  
Gina-action na rin po ng departamento ang mga reklamo nila through mass conciliations. Meron po tayong datos dyan na kung dati po, ang dinatnang po namin backlog no, nung ako yung naging na-appoint na sekretary

**[00:15:44.020] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000188)  
Ang kailangan namin action na rin was 3,000.

**[00:15:47.320] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000189)  
So na-address na po namin yan.

**[00:15:49.140] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000190)  
Although tuloy-tuloy po yung pag-file ng mga reklamo ng mga homebuyers versus the developers

**[00:15:54.740] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000191)  
Tuloy-tuloy po ang pagkakondak natin ng mga conciliations.

**[00:15:58.880] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000192)  
So ito na po ang DHSUD ngayon.

**[00:16:02.180] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000193)  
Sana po ito pong presentation namin, ito pong ina-appel na namin

**[00:16:07.040] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000194)  
na dagdag na pondo para sa mga housing programs po ng gobyerno ay inyong makonsider.

**[00:16:12.500] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000195)  
Magandang hapon po. Maraming salamat po.

**[00:16:16.980] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000196)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[00:16:18.380] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000197)  
I would just like to request first, ComSec, to read the resource person from DBM.

**[00:16:27.160] Committee Secretary** (asr-000198)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[00:16:28.480] Committee Secretary** (asr-000199)  
We would like to acknowledge our resource person, our guest from the DBM, headed by Director Benjelette Zuniga.

**[00:16:38.200] Committee Secretary** (asr-000200)  
Thank you, ma'am.

**[00:16:40.860] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000201)  
Before we proceed with interpellators, I would like to remind our distinguished colleagues

**[00:16:47.820] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000202)  
that each member is given five minutes for questioning or interpellation,

**[00:16:52.600] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000203)  
excluding the response of the resource persons.

**[00:16:55.960] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000204)  
And when you hear the bell sound, it means you only have like 30 seconds

**[00:17:00.780] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000205)  
to wind up with your questions.

**[00:17:02.620] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000206)  
So as part of our long-standing, repeated tradition of the House of Representatives

**[00:17:07.840] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000207)  
to accord preferential treatment to the minority of the House

**[00:17:13.260] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000208)  
and followed by the independent and the majority members in an alternating manner.

**[00:17:19.240] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000209)  
Can we present?

**[00:17:20.940] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000210)  
Ah, yes, nagpa-present pala.

**[00:17:22.080] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000211)  
That was just the introduction.

**[00:17:24.100] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000212)  
Okay.

**[00:17:26.500] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000213)  
I, I thought we need to dispense the presentation.

**[00:17:29.600] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000214)  
We'll just proceed with the interpellation.

**[00:17:32.500] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000215)  
Although I mentioned the summary of the presentation.

**[00:17:34.260] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000216)  
Okay, I would like to request once again,

**[00:17:36.160] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000217)  
Secretary and some other attached agencies to please proceed with your presentations.

**[00:17:41.220] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000218)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[00:17:42.340] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000219)  
Usec Henry will present.

**[00:17:44.140] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000220)  
Thank you.

**[00:17:46.497] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000220-split-1)  
Pero yun na rin yun.

**[00:17:49.585] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000221)  
Good afternoon, Madam Chair.

**[00:17:51.120] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000222)  
Former President Gloria Magal-Arroyo, distinguished members of the House Committee on Appropriations.

**[00:17:57.620] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000223)  
Today, our briefing focuses

**[00:17:59.220] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000224)  
on the Department, its key shelter agencies, and our various programs.

**[00:18:05.760] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000225)  
As an outline, we would like to cover a very brief overview of the agency's mandate,

**[00:18:11.140] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000226)  
the proposed budget highlights for 2027, our accomplishments for the year 2025 and the targets,

**[00:18:20.780] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000227)  
our performance, our budget utilization rate, as well as an additional request from our key shelter agencies.

**[00:18:29.760] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000228)  
By way of background, our department is quite young.

**[00:18:34.000] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000229)  
It's seven years old, but it is the primary national government agency or entity responsible for the management of housing,

**[00:18:41.400] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000230)  
human settlements, and urban development.

**[00:18:44.160] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000231)  
The department is also the sole and main planning and policymaking, regulatory, program coordination,

**[00:18:52.540] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000232)  
and performance monitoring entity for all housing, human settlements, and urban development.

**[00:18:59.570] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000233)  
Our attached agencies can be categorized into three.

**[00:19:03.830] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000234)  
First, for production with the National Housing Authority,

**[00:19:07.640] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000235)  
financing with the Social Housing Finance Corporation,

**[00:19:11.140] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000236)  
the Pag-ibig Fund,

**[00:19:13.240] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000237)  
and the National Home Mortgage Finance Corporation,

**[00:19:16.400] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000238)  
and our attached adjudication body, the Human Settlements Adjudication Commission, or HSAAC.

**[00:19:25.220] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000239)  
To show you the highlights and major thrusts for 2027's proposed budget,

**[00:19:31.080] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000240)  
I'd like to show you the comparison of the 2026 GAA, the 2027 NEP,

**[00:19:37.420] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000241)  
and the variance between the 2027 proposal of the DSUD and the key shelter agencies.

**[00:19:45.320] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000242)  
For 2027 NEP, the total of 5.498 billion has been allocated.

**[00:19:53.580] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000243)  
In comparison, the amount is 579 million smaller than the 2026 GAA.

**[00:20:01.960] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000244)  
In contrast, however, the total proposed submission of DSUD and its key shelter agencies

**[00:20:09.100] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000245)  
totaled approximately 60 billion,

**[00:20:11.980] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000246)  
or an eventual shortfall of 54 billion in the total amount being requested.

**[00:20:21.920] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000247)  
For DSUD, the amount that is being allocated for next year is 2.492 billion.

**[00:20:29.140] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000248)  
In contrast, the requested amount of DSUD for its various flagships,

**[00:20:33.800] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000249)  
flagships, and regular programs amount to 4.962 billion.

**[00:20:40.480] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000250)  
For the Human Settlements Adjudication Commission,

**[00:20:44.080] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000251)  
a total amount of 566 million has been requested for 2027

**[00:20:49.760] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000252)  
against a proposed budget of 890 million.

**[00:20:54.080] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000253)  
The amount are being requested for HSAAC's resolution of cases and complaints

**[00:21:00.020] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000254)  
arising from the implementation of laws, rules, and regulations

**[00:21:05.160] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000255)  
related to zoning, subdivision and condominium developments,

**[00:21:09.380] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000256)  
intra and inter homeowners association disputes, and appealed cases.

**[00:21:15.880] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000257)  
Our main production arm, the National Housing Authority,

**[00:21:19.460] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000258)  
is being allocated 2.274 billion for 2027 NEP.

**[00:21:25.720] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000259)  
In contrast, it requested a budget of 44.732 billion.

**[00:21:32.700] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000260)  
The amount requested is to...

**[00:21:35.300] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000261)  
...to support resettlement programs for displaced families,

**[00:21:39.060] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000262)  
kalami victims, indigenous peoples, including former rebels.

**[00:21:45.200] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000263)  
The Social Housing Finance Corporation, another financing agency,

**[00:21:49.780] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000264)  
was being allocated the amount of 166 million for the 2027 NEP.

**[00:21:56.430] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000265)  
In contrast, it has requested a amount of 4.666 billion.

**[00:22:02.310] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000266)  
It is the main arm agency in charge...

**[00:22:05.240] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000267)  
...with the Community Mortgage Program.

**[00:22:07.420] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000268)  
And in previous years, have also been involved in high-density housing programs

**[00:22:13.340] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000269)  
in providing support to DOTR and DPWH,

**[00:22:18.060] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000270)  
as well as provide Marawi Shelter Program.

**[00:22:23.760] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000271)  
The National Home Mortgage Finance Corporation is being allocated 450,000.

**[00:22:31.640] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000272)  
Tama po yung kanina, 450,000.

**[00:22:34.100] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000273)  
In contrast, the amount it is requesting is

**[00:22:36.740] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000274)  
P4.750 billion.

**[00:22:40.140] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000275)  
And that amount will be used to purchase receivables of various tenors,

**[00:22:45.440] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000276)  
as well as to be utilized for the securitization of its asset-backed securities.

**[00:22:52.680] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000277)  
Our last attached agency is the Home Development Mutual Fund.

**[00:22:58.380] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000278)  
They are not asking for any budget,

**[00:23:01.680] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000279)  
but we would like to highlight its contribution to the

**[00:23:05.680] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000280)  
value chain of housing.

**[00:23:08.500] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000281)  
It basically addresses the housing needs by providing rental housing loan,

**[00:23:14.240] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000282)  
as mentioned by the Secretary,

**[00:23:15.780] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000283)  
Home Loan Program, GLAD Plus,

**[00:23:19.060] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000284)  
for acquisition, including the purchase of ROPA or repossessed assets.

**[00:23:25.440] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000285)  
In addition, since last year, it has brought down its affordable housing financing

**[00:23:33.280] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000286)  
by lowering the percentage...

**[00:23:36.600] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000287)  
...or the interest to 3% for socialized housing,

**[00:23:41.040] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000288)  
4.5% for economic housing,

**[00:23:44.200] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000289)  
and 5.75% for open housing up to a maximum of 10 million pesos.

**[00:23:52.520] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000290)  
Let us now consider the accomplishments of the housing sector versus the target in 2025.

**[00:23:59.860] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000291)  
As shown in the table, we have outlined the accomplishment during the administration of President Aquino.

**[00:24:08.100] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000292)  
Just to clarify, ito pong table na ito is just the government housing program.

**[00:24:12.460] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000293)  
So we do not expect these numbers, the 1.13, to address the 3.7 million backlog by 2028

**[00:24:22.460] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000294)  
because as I said a while ago, kasama po yung private sector production to address the backlog.

**[00:24:29.120] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000295)  
Thank you.

**[00:24:30.380] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000296)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[00:24:32.280] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000297)  
So we have outlined here the accomplishments during the term of President Aquino,

**[00:24:38.100] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000298)  
President Duterte, and the current administration divided into July 2022 to the present, or July 2026,

**[00:24:47.460] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000299)  
and then the balance from August 2026 to June of 2028.

**[00:24:53.000] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000300)  
For the accomplishment for this administration,

**[00:24:57.340] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000301)  
a total of 588,757 have been accomplished, direct and indirect units or assistance.

**[00:25:06.640] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000302)  
A total of

**[00:25:08.620] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000303)  
544,000 is still to be undertaken with a target of 1.133 million by the end of this administration.

**[00:25:20.060] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000304)  
As noted in the presentation of our Secretary,

**[00:25:23.400] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000305)  
since his assumption, the 4PH has been expanded to allow more options to the Filipino families.

**[00:25:31.340] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000306)  
In contrast to the vertical housing that was previously undertaken,

**[00:25:36.380] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000307)  
today home ownership can include horizontal housing,

**[00:25:40.180] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000308)  
the real and other property acquired or ROPA,

**[00:25:44.520] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000309)  
the provision of low-interest incentive programs by Pag-IBIG,

**[00:25:49.180] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000310)  
as well as the high-density housing of the Social Housing Finance Corporation.

**[00:25:54.120] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000311)  
In terms of rental and transitional housing,

**[00:25:58.160] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000312)  
new projects are being developed and have been started,

**[00:26:01.580] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000313)  
and also modular housing for emergency and temporary housing in disaster-stricken areas.

**[00:26:10.120] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000314)  
As part of the revitalization program,

**[00:26:13.460] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000315)  
the community-led housing through the enhanced community mortgage was revived last year,

**[00:26:20.358] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000316)  
covering a period of...

**[00:26:22.687] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000317)  
to cover the hiatus of three years in the previous term, previous Secretary.

**[00:26:28.600] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000318)  
Today, we have also revitalized the presidential proclamation

**[00:26:33.460] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000319)  
and non-socialized housing enrolled under expanded 4PH

**[00:26:36.740] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000320)  
to add up to additional land and housing

**[00:26:40.140] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000322)  
supply mobilization.

**[00:26:44.540] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000323)  
The 2025 DSUD performance at a glance.

**[00:26:48.300] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000324)  
Let me now highlight some of the programs that are being undertaken by the Department.

**[00:26:53.360] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000325)  
First is the Human Settlements and Urban Development Coordination Program,

**[00:26:58.600] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000326)  
where the DSUD was able to work with different government agencies

**[00:27:03.660] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000327)  
in improving or updating the National Resettlement Policy Framework,

**[00:27:09.470] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000328)  
the incorporation of emergency shelter support,

**[00:27:13.000] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000330)  
to affected families,

**[00:27:14.460] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000331)  
the cooperation between DSUD and the SWD

**[00:27:18.920] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000332)  
for those graduating from the 4Ps to access 4PH,

**[00:27:23.240] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000333)  
and our partnership with the Department of Education

**[00:27:26.680] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000334)  
to handle the housing requirements in resettlement areas.

**[00:27:31.460] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000335)  
With the revitalization of the presidential proclamations,

**[00:27:35.220] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000336)  
a total of 213 families have been awarded the Certificate of Entitlement.

**[00:27:41.880] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000337)  
We have also implemented IDSAP,

**[00:27:44.360] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000338)  
or the Integrated Disaster Shelter Assistance Program,

**[00:27:47.280] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000339)  
and in 2025,

**[00:27:49.140] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000340)  
we're able to fund or give benefits to about 16,700 partially damaged houses,

**[00:27:56.240] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000341)  
6,550 totally damaged houses,

**[00:28:00.880] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000342)  
at the amount of approximately 365 million.

**[00:28:05.520] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000343)  
To date, all amount has been spent as of middle of the year.

**[00:28:09.680] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000344)  
In addition,

**[00:28:12.020] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000345)  
the DSUD also is mobilizing or has mobilized the National Drive

**[00:28:15.700] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000346)  
against professional squatters and squatting syndicates,

**[00:28:19.760] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000347)  
with 39 cases being processed,

**[00:28:22.440] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000348)  
12 capacity building among the various agencies,

**[00:28:25.660] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000349)  
and active local end-ups, cases of about 300.

**[00:28:31.960] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000350)  
For our Homeowners Association and Community Development Program,

**[00:28:36.920] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000351)  
the unit is principally in charge of

**[00:28:40.180] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000352)  
registering associations,

**[00:28:42.000] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000353)  
and to date,

**[00:28:43.880] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000354)  
we have a total of 2,705 registered neighborhood homeowners association,

**[00:28:50.180] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000355)  
23,698 regular homeowners association,

**[00:28:55.660] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000356)  
238 federations,

**[00:28:59.520] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000357)  
and 35 confederations.

**[00:29:02.110] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000358)  
In addition,

**[00:29:03.320] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000359)  
the Bureau was able to deliver 100% or over 100% achievement

**[00:29:08.620] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000360)  
in its capacity building,

**[00:29:10.180] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000361)  
while issuing various policies

**[00:29:13.060] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000362)  
to enhance the protection of homeowners association.

**[00:29:20.180] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000363)  
In our Housing and Real Estate Development Regulations Commission,

**[00:29:25.140] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000364)  
a total of 491 licenses to sell was issued last year,

**[00:29:30.640] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000365)  
of which 418 covers residential and 73 non-residential.

**[00:29:37.860] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000366)  
Last year, towards end of November and early December,

**[00:29:42.100] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000367)  
the Department together with the DEB promulgated

**[00:29:45.760] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000368)  
Joint Memo Circular Number 2025-001

**[00:29:49.660] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000369)  
and its Implementing Rules and Regulations

**[00:29:52.000] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000370)  
to adjust the price ceiling for socialized subdivision and condominium projects.

**[00:29:57.640] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000371)  
With the Bureau of Internal Revenue also, a Revenue Circular No. 48-025 was issued by the Bureau to streamline the requirements for the application of Certificate of Tax Exemptions for Socialized Economic Housing pursuant to Republic Act 7279 and Executive Order 226.

**[00:30:24.820] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000372)  
Upon the prodding of the President and with the full support of the Secretary and the other agencies, late June, a Joint Memo Circular No. 1-026 was signed by 15 agencies to streamline the permitting system for housing.

**[00:30:45.340] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000373)  
As shown, a number of processes and documentary requirements had been removed or will be removed as part of the streamlining process.

**[00:30:54.260] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000374)  
And the overall processing time is expected to be reduced as much as 50%.

**[00:31:02.640] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000375)  
In the aspect of environmental land use and urban planning and development program, a total of 63.5% of our LGUs now have risk-informed plans.

**[00:31:14.140] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000376)  
This number excludes the BARM as they are under the Department of Human Settlements and Development, which is outside the purview of our agency.

**[00:31:23.840] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000377)  
A total of 153.

**[00:31:26.680] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000378)  
In 2023, LGUs were also trained and provided plan smart accounts, six pilot sites were identified for AutoCAM projects, and various LGUs have been provided CLUPs and PDP, a comprehensive land use plan and provincial development, physical development plan programs, subsidy studies in local shelter plant and other.

**[00:31:50.720] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000379)  
We have also modernized our policies by the issuance of various guidelines.

**[00:31:55.900] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000380)  
On planned unit development, resilient urban design development guidelines, transit-oriented development, and open space development programs.

**[00:32:09.120] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000381)  
Key planning tools and techniques have also been updated.

**[00:32:13.380] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000382)  
Two of the major digital initiatives last year of this program includes Plan Smart, which tries to automate the digitalization of risk-informed local plans.

**[00:32:26.740] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000383)  
This is expected to reduce the planning cycle or review to less than one year from the previous two to three years.

**[00:32:34.760] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000384)  
In addition, we are now using digital remote sensing as well as geographic information system plus machine learning or AI to prepare our monitoring of compliance of land use and zoning.

**[00:32:53.960] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000385)  
This is being undertaken through our AutoCAM project.

**[00:33:00.180] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000386)  
In highlighting our budget utilization, let me highlight the following summary.

**[00:33:07.640] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000387)  
The D-SUDE has a total current budget of 1.455 billion and a continuing budget of 1.497 for a total of 2,952 million.

**[00:33:23.460] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000388)  
Of this number, 1 billion has already been obligated or about a third has been obligated.

**[00:33:31.740] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000391)  
While the obligation rate is 35%, the total disbursement rate is at 74%.

**[00:33:43.980] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000392)  
Excluding the amount for subsidy, the D-SUDE has actually exceeded the amount targeted for spending for the first half of this year.

**[00:33:55.200] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000393)  
So our rough approximate is 100.4%.

**[00:34:00.320] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000394)  
This covers all the various programs including the initiatives undertaken by other programs of the department.

**[00:34:11.860] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000395)  
When we look at the comparative budget utilization from 2021 to 2025, for us to be to Apple to Apple, we've removed actually the interest subsidy which only came in 23 and 24.

**[00:34:27.900] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000396)  
So we have an average allocation of...

**[00:34:31.760] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000397)  
Of which 86.82 overall obligation rate and a 95.37 overall disbursement rate.

**[00:34:43.360] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000398)  
Before we end, we'd like to mention that the D-SUDE has, under this administration, been able to receive three consecutive period of unmodified opinion from the department or from the commission on audit for the year 2023-2024.

**[00:35:02.320] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000399)  
and 2025.

**[00:35:05.240] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000400)  
Before we end, may we request the consideration of our committee to some of the additional budget requests being considered.

**[00:35:13.960] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000401)  
The D-SUDE is proposing additional budget of 280 million to cover ISSP, the implementation and rollout of AutoCAM, the support to the Marawi Board based on Republic Act 11696,

**[00:35:31.240] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000402)  
the creation

**[00:35:32.720] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000403)  
of an anti-illegal real estate practice interagency task force

**[00:35:37.260] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000404)  
and the second phase of the network of open green space

**[00:35:41.780] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000405)  
and public parks projects.

**[00:35:44.440] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000406)  
In addition, we would like to support the request

**[00:35:48.260] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000407)  
of our attached agencies, AITSAC,

**[00:35:50.640] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000408)  
for 47 million to cover its COS personnel

**[00:35:55.820] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000409)  
and motor vehicle procurement,

**[00:35:58.480] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000410)  
the NHA of 1.623 billion

**[00:36:02.460] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000411)  
for its comprehensive and integrated housing program

**[00:36:05.640] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000412)  
covering danger areas and SC Mandamus,

**[00:36:09.980] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000413)  
a amount of 2 billion for the Social Housing Finance Corporation

**[00:36:13.760] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000414)  
for its enhanced community mortgage program,

**[00:36:17.120] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000415)  
and a amount of 8.08 billion broken down to 4.75 million

**[00:36:24.080] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000416)  
for the receivables purchase

**[00:36:25.980] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000417)  
and 3.33 billion

**[00:36:28.040] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000418)  
for the payment of NHMFC's SSS obligation.

**[00:36:39.160] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000419)  
In conclusion, what does our 2027 budget entail

**[00:36:44.960] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000420)  
or will be able to do?

**[00:36:47.720] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000421)  
One, it will be able to accelerate housing delivery

**[00:36:50.940] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000422)  
and expand the pathways beyond the previous single modality.

**[00:36:55.440] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000423)  
Second, it will allow us to mobilize

**[00:36:57.360] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000424)  
the whole of the housing finance system

**[00:37:00.400] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000425)  
including Pag-IBIG, the Social Housing Finance Corporation,

**[00:37:04.180] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000426)  
and NHMFC to multiply the effect of government funding.

**[00:37:08.980] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000427)  
Three, improve the location and quality of settlements

**[00:37:12.640] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000428)  
by scaling up Planado and the risk-informed

**[00:37:16.000] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000429)  
comprehensive land use plans

**[00:37:17.700] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000430)  
while strengthening regulation and community governance,

**[00:37:22.100] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000431)  
thus reducing processing backlogs

**[00:37:24.720] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000432)  
and improve consumer and homeowners association services.

**[00:37:28.660] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000433)  
And finally, the resolution of specific bottlenecks

**[00:37:32.200] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000434)  
responsible for consumer and home owners association services.

**[00:37:33.660] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000435)  
We are also responsible for continuing appropriation

**[00:37:34.840] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000436)  
and closely monitoring our 2027 delivery.

**[00:37:38.980] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000437)  
On that note, we appreciate the committee's continued support

**[00:37:42.500] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000438)  
in advancing the dignified housing and sustainable communities

**[00:37:46.240] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000439)  
and we look forward to working closely with the stakeholders

**[00:37:49.500] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000440)  
for a safe, resilient, and inclusive housing sector.

**[00:37:54.220] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-000441)  
Thank you very much.

**[00:37:56.820] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000442)  
Thank you.

**[00:37:57.720] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000443)  
I thought all the while Chairperson Miro Quimbo is here.

**[00:38:02.500] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000444)  
He's having his

**[00:38:03.520] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000445)  
meeting with committee on

**[00:38:05.780] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000446)  
ways and means. Naghanap ng pondo

**[00:38:08.020] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000447)  
baka may pangdagdag tayo.

**[00:38:10.460] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000448)  
I'm moving forward.

**[00:38:13.900] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000449)  
First to interpelate, of course,

**[00:38:15.800] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000450)  
from the minority,

**[00:38:16.840] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000451)  
Assistant Minority Leader, Sarah Jane

**[00:38:19.900] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000452)  
Ilago.

**[00:38:22.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000453)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Chair.

**[00:38:24.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000454)  
Tungkol sa qualifications

**[00:38:25.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000455)  
na pwedeng mag-apply para sa

**[00:38:27.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000456)  
expanded 4PH program,

**[00:38:29.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000457)  
yung isa sa mga requirement

**[00:38:31.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000458)  
natin ay dapat

**[00:38:33.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000459)  
niyembro ng pag-ibig

**[00:38:34.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000460)  
at nagko-contribute

**[00:38:37.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000461)  
for 12 months.

**[00:38:39.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000462)  
Tama ho ba?

**[00:38:40.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000463)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[00:38:42.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000464)  
Ang concern namin dito, Madam Chair,

**[00:38:45.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000465)  
marami ho dun sa mga pinaka

**[00:38:47.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000466)  
nangangailangan nating mga kababayan.

**[00:38:49.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000467)  
Wala rin namang trabaho.

**[00:38:50.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000468)  
Kahit po yung voluntary payors

**[00:38:53.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000469)  
na minimum

**[00:38:55.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000470)  
nasa 200 pesos per month,

**[00:38:57.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000471)  
hindi pa rin po ganun kadali.

**[00:38:58.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000472)  
Lalong-lalo na dun sa mga pinaka walang-wala

**[00:39:01.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000473)  
nating mga kababayan.

**[00:39:03.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000474)  
Kaya ho, meron ho bang

**[00:39:05.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000475)  
mga efforts

**[00:39:08.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000476)  
ngayon para ma-review

**[00:39:09.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000477)  
itong qualifications na ito

**[00:39:11.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000478)  
at matiyak natin na yung mga pinaka

**[00:39:13.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000479)  
nangangailangan nating mga kababayan

**[00:39:15.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000480)  
na low income

**[00:39:17.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000481)  
o kaya minimum wage earners

**[00:39:19.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000482)  
at walang regular na trabaho

**[00:39:20.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000483)  
ay makaka-avail sa programa na ito.

**[00:39:24.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000484)  
Salamat po.

**[00:39:26.598] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000485)  
Can I?

**[00:39:27.627] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000486)  
Secretary, may answer?

**[00:39:28.340] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000487)  
Ang isa po ito sa mga rason kaya natin

**[00:39:31.220] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000488)  
in-expand ang pambansang pabahay

**[00:39:33.120] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000489)  
para sa Pilipino Program or 4PH.

**[00:39:37.140] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000490)  
Yes, tama po yung sinabi ninyo

**[00:39:40.100] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000491)  
na para po siya makautang sa pag-ibig,

**[00:39:43.260] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000492)  
kailangan member siya ng pag-ibig

**[00:39:45.180] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000493)  
and para ka maging member ng pag-ibig,

**[00:39:47.120] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000494)  
dapat may trabaho ka.

**[00:39:49.220] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000495)  
So, in-expand po natin yung 4PH

**[00:39:51.320] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000496)  
dito sa mga hindi magka-qualify

**[00:39:52.900] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000497)  
sa mga nasabi natin mga requirements.

**[00:39:55.600] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000498)  
Nandito po papasok yung ibang modalities

**[00:39:57.840] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000499)  
ng 4PH.

**[00:39:59.080] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000500)  
Nandito po yung ating sinisigilan

**[00:40:01.480] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000501)  
ngayon na rental housing modality

**[00:40:03.220] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000502)  
and yung community mortgage program.

**[00:40:06.330] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000503)  
Yun po ang nakita nating solusyon.

**[00:40:09.410] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000504)  
Doon sa mga kababayan natin,

**[00:40:11.620] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000505)  
parang transition lang po ito, no?

**[00:40:13.200] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000506)  
Habang hindi pa po sila ready bumili

**[00:40:15.360] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000507)  
ng kanilang mga tahanan o bahay,

**[00:40:18.580] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000508)  
mayroon pong ibang programa ang gobyerno.

**[00:40:20.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000509)  
Sa nabanggit nyo po na ibang mga programa,

**[00:40:22.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000510)  
magkano ho kada buwan

**[00:40:24.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000511)  
yung mga kailangan bayaran

**[00:40:26.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000512)  
pagdating sa mga programa na ito?

**[00:40:28.260] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000513)  
Una-una po,

**[00:40:29.220] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000514)  
ang community mortgage program,

**[00:40:31.360] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000515)  
nagre-range po yan ng

**[00:40:34.280] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000516)  
on the average,

**[00:40:35.680] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000517)  
600 to 800 pesos

**[00:40:37.160] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000518)  
per month.

**[00:40:39.120] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000519)  
Ang rental po natin ay

**[00:40:41.080] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000520)  
below 1,500 pesos.

**[00:40:43.000] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000521)  
Yan po yung intention natin dito

**[00:40:45.300] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000522)  
sa mga pilot rental housing projects

**[00:40:47.280] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000523)  
natin na kasulukuyang

**[00:40:49.400] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000524)  
itinatayo natin.

**[00:40:50.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000525)  
Madam Chair, sa ngayon,

**[00:40:52.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000526)  
ilan na po yung mga nag-apply

**[00:40:54.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000527)  
o yung kasulukuyan na nandito na

**[00:40:57.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000528)  
dito sa mga nabanggit nyo

**[00:40:59.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000529)  
na ibang modalities?

**[00:41:01.180] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000530)  
Pagdating po sa community mortgage program,

**[00:41:02.960] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000531)  
ahayaan ko pong magbigay ng sagot

**[00:41:05.320] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000532)  
si SHFC CEO Rico Laxa.

**[00:41:09.620] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000533)  
Yes, President Laxa, you are recognized.

**[00:41:12.740] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000534)  
Madam Chair,

**[00:41:14.960] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000535)  
for the information of the body

**[00:41:17.060] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000536)  
sa informasyon po ng,

**[00:41:18.100] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000537)  
para sa informasyon ni Cong. Lagu,

**[00:41:21.020] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000538)  
mula po nung i-relaunch namin

**[00:41:23.200] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000539)  
yung enhanced community mortgage program

**[00:41:25.620] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000540)  
in July last year,

**[00:41:27.800] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000541)  
nakapag-approve na po tayo

**[00:41:29.380] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000542)  
ng 47 projects.

**[00:41:30.680] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000543)  
At ito po ay

**[00:41:32.920] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000544)  
magbibigay ng

**[00:41:33.840] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000545)  
benepisyo sa halos sa

**[00:41:36.880] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000546)  
9,000 families.

**[00:41:38.160] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000547)  
So, ang proseso po namin

**[00:41:41.160] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000548)  
ay napakadali na po.

**[00:41:42.460] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000549)  
In fact, on the average po,

**[00:41:44.340] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000550)  
naglalaro sa 10

**[00:41:46.540] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000551)  
working days lang ang processing

**[00:41:48.900] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000552)  
ng CMP for as long

**[00:41:50.820] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000553)  
as

**[00:42:24.880] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000569)

**[00:42:26.160] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000571)

**[00:42:29.160] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000572)

**[00:42:31.060] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000573)

**[00:43:01.700] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000585)

**[00:43:03.920] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000586)

**[00:43:05.580] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000587)

**[00:43:07.740] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000588)

**[00:43:09.840] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000589)

**[00:43:12.060] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000590)

**[00:43:14.020] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000591)

**[00:43:15.940] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000592)
