**[00:43:18.220] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000593)  
60 applications.

**[00:43:20.300] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000594)  
Ah, yung 37 po dito,

**[00:43:22.140] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000595)  
eh, medyo maganda na yung

**[00:43:23.940] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000596)  
ah, ah, halos

**[00:43:25.960] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000597)  
ready na rin po yung kanilang

**[00:43:27.940] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000598)  
implementation.

**[00:43:29.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000599)  
Ano sa tingin niyo yung total need

**[00:43:31.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000600)  
na nirequest niyo na hindi

**[00:43:34.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000601)  
bibigyan ng pondo for the next year?

**[00:43:35.380] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000602)  
Yung pong 37, sa estimate po

**[00:43:37.980] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000603)  
natin, yung mga about 1.6 billion

**[00:43:39.860] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-000604)  
ang pangangailangan namin.

**[00:43:45.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000605)  
Pinag-aaralan na rin ba ng DSUD na

**[00:43:47.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000606)  
mapababa, no, yung

**[00:43:49.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000607)  
iba namang mga options?

**[00:43:51.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000608)  
Halimbawa po, dito sa Palayan City

**[00:43:53.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000609)  
Township, na nagkakahalagan ng

**[00:43:56.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000610)  
1.3 million pesos, na may

**[00:43:58.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000611)  
buwanang bayad na 4,900 pesos.

**[00:44:01.137] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000612)  
Ah,

**[00:44:02.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000613)  
isa ho sa na-raise nating mga concern nitong mga

**[00:44:04.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000614)  
nakaraan na budget deliberations,

**[00:44:05.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000615)  
na kung pwede bang pag-aaralan

**[00:44:07.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000616)  
yung mas mataas na subsidy ng government

**[00:44:09.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000617)  
para mas mapababa yung presyo na ito?

**[00:44:13.060] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000618)  
Ah, Madam Chair,

**[00:44:14.060] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000619)  
ang ginawa po natin dyan,

**[00:44:15.520] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000620)  
dalawa po ang bagay na ginawa natin

**[00:44:18.120] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000621)  
para bumaba yung

**[00:44:19.360] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000622)  
monthly amortization. Number one,

**[00:44:22.200] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000623)  
nag-introduce po tayo, instead

**[00:44:23.980] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000624)  
na yung subsidy po ang dinagdaga natin,

**[00:44:26.800] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000625)  
interest subsidy po kasi yun eh,

**[00:44:28.240] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000626)  
yung original na subsidy.

**[00:44:29.280] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000627)  
So ang ginawa po natin, binabaan po

**[00:44:32.200] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000628)  
natin yung interest mismo para maging

**[00:44:34.220] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000629)  
sustainable. Kasi naging

**[00:44:36.080] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000630)  
problema po nung unang programa, dahil

**[00:44:38.060] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000631)  
oversubsidized tayo sa aming

**[00:44:39.540] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000632)  
opinion, 5% ang ating

**[00:44:42.260] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000633)  
sinasubsidized. Pag nagpunta

**[00:44:44.380] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000634)  
po yung home buyer o yung

**[00:44:46.180] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000635)  
beneficiary sa mga financial institutions,

**[00:44:48.640] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000636)  
dahil hindi naman po

**[00:44:50.100] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000637)  
ito batas, itong 5%

**[00:44:52.500] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000638)  
na subsidy, tingin nila hindi sustainable,

**[00:44:55.200] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000639)  
inevaluate pa rin nila

**[00:44:55.720] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000640)  
yung mga buyers sa 6%.

**[00:44:57.960] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000641)  
So marami pong mataas po yung rejection rate. So ang ginawa po natin through the efforts po ng pag-ibig fund, ibinaba po natin from 6.25% ang interest po ng socialized housing, binaba po natin ng 3%.

**[00:45:11.240] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000642)  
Ngayon ang subsidy po natin is 2%. Dati po 5%, nag-i-end up sila ng 1%. Ngayon po dahil 3% na lang, ang subsidy nilang natin is 2%. Ang dulo pa rin po nila is 1%.

**[00:45:23.020] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000643)  
Pangalawa, kung hindi pa rin po kaya, yung sinasabi, tulad po ng halimbawa nga sinabi nyo po kanina na 4,000, meron po nga, pwede po sila magpa-approve sa pag-ibig fund on a project basis, nung tinatawag natin graduated amortization.

**[00:45:38.760] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000644)  
More or less, yung unang taon niya, kung 4,000 yung level amortization, nung binili niya pong bahay na 4,000, ang unang hulog lang niya ng taon, sa unang taon, ay kalahati.

**[00:45:52.500] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000645)  
More or less kalahati, 2,000. Then it is gradually increased yearly, hanggang ang assumption natin, nagkakaroon siya ng dagdag na nakakaprepara po siya, yung mga kapamilya niya tumutulong na po.

**[00:46:06.540] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000646)  
Then, unti-unti po yung pagtaas ng monthly amortization. So yun po ang naging solution natin dun sa mga nangangailangan pa ng mas mababang monthly amortization.

**[00:46:17.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000647)  
Pwede po ba yung 4,900 per month? Yun na yung sakop ng mas pinababa ninyo?

**[00:46:23.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000648)  
So, in simple terms po, magkano na po ngayon yung pinakamababang pwede?

**[00:46:28.060] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000649)  
Kalahati po nun.

**[00:46:29.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000650)  
Nasa kalahati?

**[00:46:30.312] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000651)  
Opo.

**[00:46:30.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000652)  
Alright. Sige. Pagdating naman po sa pacing ng construction, for 2025, out of 100,000 units, nasa 34,000 na po tayo so far, yung natatapos natin.

**[00:46:42.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000653)  
And then for 2024, from 75,000 units na target, 24 po yung naipatayo. Maari niyo po bang maibahagi muli sa committee?

**[00:46:50.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000654)  
Ano ba yung mga factors na nakaka-apekto sa mababang utilization natin ng pondo?

**[00:46:56.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000655)  
At saan ba yung bara at doon sa nakaka-apekto sa pagbagal mismo ng construction at implementation?

**[00:47:04.360] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000656)  
Madam Chair, ito pong more or less 100,000, ito po yung inventory. Tapos na po ito.

**[00:47:11.340] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000657)  
Yung 30,000 po na binigit ninyo, yun yung actual take-out. Ibig sabihin yun yung nag-loan, na-approve yung loan.

**[00:47:18.120] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000658)  
So, ano yung pangalawa, ma'am?

**[00:47:19.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000659)  
Ma'am, ano po yung pangalawang tanong?

**[00:47:24.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000660)  
Paano po natin mapapabilis yung process ng mga turnover dito sa mga nagawa ng unit?

**[00:47:32.340] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000661)  
Noong 2025 po, ang taken-out units was only 1,000. Actually, yun yung 4PH na numbers noong 2025 before we expanded the program.

**[00:47:45.440] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000662)  
Ngayon po, nasa almost 130,000 yung inventory ng 4PH.

**[00:47:52.160] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000663)  
Ito po yung modality na binibili. Ito po yung vertical, mga condominium, and horizontal, yung mga house and lot na kasama po sa 4PH.

**[00:48:02.000] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000664)  
So, makikita po natin, more than 120 times po ang in-increase ng production in terms of inventory ng 4PH.

**[00:48:11.040] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000665)  
Ngayon, paano po ito mapapabilis?

**[00:48:13.620] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000666)  
In-address po natin, tulad po ng sabi ko, ang ka-partner po natin dito sa Pambansang Pabahay para sa Pilipino Program

**[00:48:20.060] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000667)  
are the private sector developers.

**[00:48:21.860] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000668)  
who are developing socialized housing projects sa country.

**[00:48:28.160] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000669)  
So, noong binuksan po natin yung 3%, na-address po natin dyan, yung 3% interest program po, 4PH interest program po yan,

**[00:48:35.840] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000670)  
subsidized interest po yan kasi 6.25 naging 3 ng pag-ibig fund, na-address po natin yung tinatawag natin demand side.

**[00:48:43.820] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000671)  
So, mas marami na pong mga developers ngayon na nag-de-develop na po ng socialized housing.

**[00:48:51.580] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000672)  
Kasi, napakaganda po, nakita po nila, napakaganda po nung market na ito.

**[00:48:56.620] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000673)  
Ang maganda po kasi sa socialized housing market, hindi ito yung mga bumibili para mag-invest.

**[00:49:01.720] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000674)  
Ito yung bumibili dahil kailangan nila yung mga bahay nila.

**[00:49:04.740] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000675)  
So, dahil meron po tayong housing needs all over the country, basta nagtayo po tayo ng tamang produkto,

**[00:49:12.220] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000676)  
ibig sabihin po natin tamang produkto, tamang lokasyon, nandyan po yung transportation, nandyan yung mga support facilities,

**[00:49:18.700] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000677)  
mabilis po nabibenta yung mga socialized housing.

**[00:49:21.700] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000678)  
So, in the next, ito pong taon na to, marami na po ang ongoing na construction ng socialized housing all over the country.

**[00:49:31.100] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000679)  
Marami na pong mga developers ang nag-inquire sa atin kung paano natin sila matutulungan.

**[00:49:36.500] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000680)  
Ang isa pong tulong dito, pag pumunta po sila sa pag-ibig fund, napibigay po sa kanyala,

**[00:49:41.820] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000681)  
saan yung areas na may mga member na nagahanap po ng mga bahay.

**[00:49:45.500] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000682)  
So, dun sila ngayon nagagawa, market driven na po ang 4PH ngayon.

**[00:49:50.240] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000683)  
Kaya sigurado, pag nagtayo sila, meron pong ito magiging takers.

**[00:49:54.740] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000684)  
So, ang tingin po natin dito, ang tingin po natin dito, yung programa po ng 4PH na sa tamang direction na po,

**[00:50:06.040] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000685)  
kailangan lang pabilisin, kailangan dagdagan ng pondo,

**[00:50:08.840] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000686)  
mas maraming dapat developers magtiwala sa programa para pumasok po dito sa 4PH program natin na ito.

**[00:50:16.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000687)  
Madam Chair, may sagot na po ba ang DHSUD dito sa COA audit report?

**[00:50:22.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000688)  
ng 2025 tungkol sa payments for interest and non-interest subsidy na umabot sa 66.7 million pesos

**[00:50:31.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000689)  
na sinasabi po ng COA na walang independent validation at supporting documents.

**[00:50:37.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000690)  
Nasagot na po ba natin ang issue dito?

**[00:50:38.880] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000691)  
Madam Chair, nasagot na po yan and close na po yung case na yan.

**[00:50:42.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000692)  
Alright, maraming salamat po.

**[00:50:44.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000693)  
Pagdating naman dito sa delays sa pagbibigay ng emergency shelter support

**[00:50:49.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000694)  
sa ilalim ng Integrated Disaster Shelter Assistance Program,

**[00:50:54.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000695)  
natugunan na po ba natin yung mga issue sa delays para matiyak na mabilis din nating natutulungan yung ating mga kababayan?

**[00:51:02.560] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000696)  
For our DSUD-IDSA program that has been addressed, there were issuances already on simplification of process.

**[00:51:10.360] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000697)  
However, hanggang last year lang po kami nabigyan ng pondo, this year hindi na po kami nabigyan ng pondo,

**[00:51:15.940] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-000698)  
and the new funds are now with NHEA.

**[00:51:19.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000699)  
Magkano ho ang funds na yun for 2026 and the proposal?

**[00:51:23.660] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000700)  
Just for 2027.

**[00:51:25.980] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000701)  
Madam Chair, for the AIDSAP po, it's with NHEA but unfortunately for both AIDSAP po and EHOP,

**[00:51:33.680] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000702)  
we haven't still received the SARO from the DBM. We're still waiting pa po for the SARO.

**[00:51:40.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000703)  
Ano hong challenge dito pagdating sa, bakit wala pa hong binibigay?

**[00:51:44.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000704)  
Kasi diba dito rin natin kinukuha halimbawa yung mga kababayan natin na nasunugan na nangangailangan ng dagdag assistance

**[00:51:51.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000705)  
o biktima

**[00:51:52.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000706)  
ng calamities o ng pagbaha.

**[00:51:56.300] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000707)  
Tama po.

**[00:51:56.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000708)  
Ito ho yung pinagkukunan natin ng dagdag pantulong sa kanila.

**[00:51:59.720] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-000709)  
Tama po yan. We're constantly following up with DBM but unfortunately I cannot speak in behalf of DBM.

**[00:52:06.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000710)  
Madam Chair, do we have a representative from DBM here who can explain to this committee?

**[00:52:13.780] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000711)  
May we request Director Suniga to please answer?

**[00:52:33.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000712)  
Madam Chair, in the interest of time, I'd just like to request for a written explanation from DBM on this issue.

**[00:52:43.620] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000713)  
Okay, Mr. DBM, you are requested to please submit your written explanation.

**[00:52:53.240] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000714)  
At tungkol dyan, Madam Chair, nais ko rin pong humingi ng paliwanag.

**[00:52:58.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000715)  
Halimbawa, sa mga kaso ng mga sunog sa urban poor communities, meron kayong mga nagiging tulungan sa local government units,

**[00:53:06.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000716)  
para matulungan yung mga affected.

**[00:53:08.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000717)  
Halimbawa po dito sa Quezon City, meron kayong active na tulungan with the Quezon City Local Government Unit.

**[00:53:14.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000718)  
Kung sa Manila po ba, meron din po kasi nagsunod-sunod din po yung matitinding sunog at pagkasira ng mga kabahayan,

**[00:53:23.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000719)  
lalong-lalo na sa Parola Compound.

**[00:53:25.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000720)  
Meron din po ba kayong active na pakipagtulungan sa LGU o may plano rin po ba para sa isang on-site or in-city relocation

**[00:53:34.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000721)  
para sa mga residente na affected?

**[00:53:39.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000722)  
In general po, basta meron pong pangangailangan ng isang community o isang LGU.

**[00:53:44.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000723)  
Ang naitutulong po ng DSUD sa ka-attached agency are the following.

**[00:53:48.840] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000724)  
Number one, nagpapadala po kami ng mga construction materials.

**[00:53:52.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000725)  
Number two, meron po tayong katulad daw na nabanggit niya ng IDSAP na nasa NHA na ngayon.

**[00:53:58.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000726)  
Unfortunately, hindi lang funded.

**[00:54:00.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000727)  
Then pangatlo, meron na po tayong transitory materials.

**[00:54:03.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000728)  
So transitory housing, temporary housing, naglalagay po tayo ng mga modular shelter units

**[00:54:10.570] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000729)  
basta meron pong lupa na ipoprovide yung LGU.

**[00:54:15.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000730)  
So yun po yung tatlong tulong na pinaprovide natin sa mga communities na nabanggit po ninyo.

**[00:54:24.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000731)  
Maraming salamat po. Hingi na lang din po tayo ng status report tungkol dito sa mga nangangailangan din ng tulong pagdating sa relocation.

**[00:54:33.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000732)  
At ang panawagan nila Madam Chair talagang on-site or in-city para malapit din sa lugar ng trabaho at paaralan ng kanilang mga anak.

**[00:54:41.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000733)  
Panghuling katanungan ko na lang, dito sa target na 1.13 million housing units sa ilalim po ng Marcos administration, nasaan na po tayo sa pag-abot nito?

**[00:54:52.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000734)  
More than 50% na po tayo as reported by the President during the last SONA.

**[00:54:57.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000735)  
And we are confident that we will be able to meet the 1.13 million commitment.

**[00:55:04.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000736)  
By the end term of the current administration.

**[00:55:08.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000737)  
And I just want to again reiterate that this 1.13 million is not expected to solve the 3.7 backlog.

**[00:55:19.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000738)  
There are other private developers who will also do their own production to cover the housing backlog.

**[00:55:27.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000739)  
So what about the production by the NHA po?

**[00:55:30.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000740)  
Nakita ko kasi isa din sila sa may pinakamalaking budget.

**[00:55:32.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000741)  
May pinakamalaking budget cut for the next year.

**[00:55:36.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000742)  
Ang sa akin lang ho, sana hindi lang po tayo nakaasa sa mga private developers.

**[00:55:40.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000743)  
Pero yung sarili din natin na capacity para magtayo ng mga housing units para sa mga nangailangan ay nasusuportahan natin.

**[00:55:50.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000744)  
In line with that Madam Chair, since wala na po akong oras,

**[00:55:54.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000745)  
hingin ko na lang po yung masasabi dito ng NHA since natanggalan po sila ng malaking chunk ng pondo.

**[00:56:01.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000746)  
At hingin ko na rin ho yung listahan ng mga private developers.

**[00:56:05.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000747)  
Kung lahat ba yan performing, lahat ba yan nagde-deliver.

**[00:56:10.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000748)  
Meron ba tayong mga napanagot?

**[00:56:11.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000749)  
Halimbawa kung merong under performance sa paggawa ho ng mga housing units.

**[00:56:18.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000750)  
Ito po ay hingin natin mula sa DSUD para maisubmit na sa committee na ito.

**[00:56:22.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000751)  
Maraming salamat Madam Chair.

**[00:56:25.580] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000752)

**[00:56:55.880] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000761)  
So may SARO, walang cash. Sabi ni DBM daw.

**[00:56:59.780] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-000762)  
Assistant Director Lulu Vizpo.

**[00:57:07.100] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-000764)  
Yung request po tungkol sa NCA, wala po po silang request.

**[00:57:10.980] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-000765)  
And then when it comes to IHAP, relative to IHAP po, this was requested last April.

**[00:57:20.040] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-000766)  
And then we submit, since this is a congressional insertion,

**[00:57:25.020] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-000767)  
this was submitted to the Office of the President.

**[00:57:27.940] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-000768)  
So up to now, wala pa po sa aming binabalik.

**[00:57:31.160] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-000769)

**[00:57:41.040] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-000770)  
So right now po, ongoing po yung process.

**[00:57:43.740] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-000771)  
So baka within this week po, ilabas po namin yung request po na yun.

**[00:57:49.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000772)  
May I just comment briefly, Madam Chair, on that response?

**[00:57:54.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000773)  
Ang sa akin lang ho, pagdating dito sa emergency shelter support,

**[00:57:57.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000774)

**[00:58:00.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000775)

**[00:58:08.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000776)

**[00:58:11.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000777)  
Sunod-sunod yung sunog sa mga communities.

**[00:58:13.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000778)

**[00:58:16.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000779)

**[00:58:22.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000780)

**[00:58:27.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000781)

**[00:58:31.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000782)  
And I just like to reiterate, Madam Chair, my request for the list of banks and private developers.

ang D-SHUD right now sa NEP

**[01:12:09.510] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001158)

**[01:12:11.070] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001159)

**[01:12:19.290] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001164)

**[01:12:22.400] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001165)

**[01:12:24.240] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001166)

**[01:12:26.250] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001167)

**[01:12:29.240] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001168)

**[01:12:35.800] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001171)

**[01:12:54.270] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001177)

**[01:13:03.430] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001180)  
Now, they provided actually,

**[01:13:05.380] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001181)  
the urban poor communities that this representation

**[01:13:08.410] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001182)  
or my office has been working with,

**[01:13:10.670] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001183)  
provided a list of socialized housing projects

**[01:13:15.430] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001184)  
and presidential proclamations that continue to be riddled with

**[01:13:18.970] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001185)

**[01:13:20.550] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001186)

**[01:13:24.650] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001187)  
or decades-long, decades-long delays

**[01:13:27.630] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001188)  
in the release of titles

**[01:13:29.610] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001189)  
and certificates of land allocation.

**[01:13:32.200] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001190)  
Even the lack of basic services,

**[01:13:34.090] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001191)  
the lack of funding for land acquisition.

**[01:13:36.540] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001192)  
So may I respectfully ask that

**[01:13:39.630] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001193)  
to furnish the good Secretary with this list.

**[01:13:42.640] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001194)  
And we would like to request that they provide

**[01:13:45.390] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001195)

**[01:13:49.160] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001196)  
Including the following information for each area.

**[01:13:51.640] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001197)  
Number one, the estimated timeline for completion.

**[01:13:56.080] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001198)  
Number two, whether or not the area is covered

**[01:13:58.640] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001199)  
by the 2027 budget in the NEP of DSUD

**[01:14:02.660] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001200)  
and its key shelter agencies.

**[01:14:04.710] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001201)

**[01:14:08.930] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001202)  
in the NET, bakit ho hindi nila sinama?

**[01:14:12.760] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001203)  
Ano ho bang mga problema o concerns ang mga

**[01:14:16.560] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001204)  
ah,

**[01:14:18.160] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001205)  
kaakibat nito?

**[01:14:19.770] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001206)

**[01:14:22.600] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001207)  
in spite of the long delays?

**[01:14:25.720] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001208)  
May I also talk about the Manggahan Residences

**[01:14:29.560] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001209)  
floodway in Pasig?

**[01:14:31.120] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001210)

**[01:14:34.600] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001211)  
Only 10 of which had been occupied.

**[01:14:37.420] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001212)  
Since the community moved in, they have raised several concerns

**[01:14:41.380] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001213)  
on the substandard quality of the buildings

**[01:14:44.010] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001214)  
which many repairs, with many repairs, necessary.

**[01:14:48.080] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001215)  
Does the NHA have funds allotted for the estate management

**[01:14:52.950] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001216)

**[01:14:56.030] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001217)  
And if yes,

**[01:14:57.700] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001218)  
How can the homeowner associations access these funds?

**[01:15:03.150] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001219)  
Pwede ba sabihin sa amin yun?

**[01:15:06.020] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001220)  
Ang daming with, there's like three questions, right?

**[01:15:09.370] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001221)  
First, we need to answer, Secretary, what's the status of the Presidential Proclamation?

**[01:15:15.140] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001222)  
Madam Chair, we will submit the status of each item in the list.

**[01:15:19.340] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001223)  
If we can be provided.

**[01:15:20.080] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001224)  
We will provide you now with the list, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:15:22.800] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001225)  
But I would just like to inform everyone, even if may list tayo, bakit ang dami at saka hindi na ayos?

**[01:15:30.700] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001226)  
Dahil sa pagpakaalam ko when I went and visited sa Iloilo, we have nine plus three proclamations.

**[01:15:37.890] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001227)  
Ang problema, hindi ito maka-move on dahil kailangan i-resurvey ang mga areas.

**[01:15:45.250] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001228)  
Because some other proclamations, the Presidential Proclamation, matagal na.

**[01:15:49.420] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001229)  
So sa masela, ang problema, hindi na makaproceed sa payment, how much amount.

**[01:15:54.900] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001230)  
Dahil una, kailangan talaga natin i-resurvey.

**[01:15:58.490] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001231)  
So we need not only the list of proclamations, but this department needs budget for the survey.

**[01:16:05.390] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001232)  
Ang tanong ko, Secretary, do you have budget for the resurveying of this area na under Presidential Proclamation?

**[01:16:14.860] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001233)  
Unang-una po, ito pong Presidential Proclamation, nahinto po itong pag-processor nito ng unang tatlong taon.

**[01:16:23.510] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001234)  
So ang sinimal nila lang ulat.

**[01:16:25.870] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001235)  
May proseso noong June 2025.

**[01:16:29.480] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001236)  
Yung po ang unang dahilan, kaya hindi po masyadong umusad ng Presidential Proclamation.

**[01:16:34.200] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001237)  
Suspended po ito ng tatlong taon na noong in-expand po natin yung 4PH, re-revive natin,

**[01:16:40.580] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001238)  
ginawa natin isang modality ng 4PH, yung Presidential Proclamation.

**[01:16:45.420] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001239)  
With regards to the budget,

**[01:16:46.700] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001240)  
Anong sabi sa?

**[01:16:48.480] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001241)  
Humingi ba tayo?

**[01:16:50.670] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001242)  
Hindi, humingi ba tayo?

**[01:16:53.300] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001243)  
Nabigyan tayo?

**[01:16:57.580] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001244)  
Magkano hiningi natin?

**[01:17:00.660] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001245)  
Meron meron.

**[01:17:01.350] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001246)  
Meron.

**[01:17:01.840] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001247)  
With regards to budget, meron daw 3 million ngayon for the service.

**[01:17:08.300] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001248)  
Diyos ko, ang budget for...

**[01:17:16.580] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001249)  
What kind of a budget is that for such a project?

**[01:17:21.780] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001250)  
These are only, we asked for 151 million, then we were given through the NEP 3 million.

**[01:17:31.380] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001251)  
Ang hiningan lang po namin, iba-iba po kasi ang administrators ng Presidential Proclamation.

**[01:17:37.120] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001252)  
So yung under sa DHSUD po, yun po yung hininga namin ng budget.

**[01:17:41.800] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001253)  
Yung iba pong Presidential Proclamation, may iba pong ahensya na nag-administer po yan.

**[01:17:46.780] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001254)  
Can you tell us bakit ganon may mga iba-ibang ahensya ang nagmamanage ng mga Presidential Proclamation?

**[01:17:55.120] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001255)  
Can it not be just one or interagency?

**[01:17:59.800] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-001256)  
It all depends po how the proclamation was issued.

**[01:18:03.390] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-001257)  
So there are those issued by...

**[01:18:06.890] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-001258)  
By DHSUD or before HADC.

**[01:18:10.580] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-001259)  
Meron din po NHA, meron DND, DSWD, may SPDA, may DNR, DOTR.

**[01:18:15.280] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-001260)  
Iba-iba po yun.

**[01:18:15.700] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-001261)  
Depende po sa pagsulat, pag-issue po ng proclamation.

**[01:18:25.040] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001262)  
Not proposed now that there should be some sort of an integrated process, interagency tasking.

**[01:18:35.446] DHSUD Undersecretary Eduardo P. Robles Jr.** (asr-001263)  
Madam Chair...

**[01:18:36.475] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001264)  
Puwede ho bang baguhin yun?

**[01:18:38.230] DHSUD Undersecretary Eduardo P. Robles Jr.** (asr-001265)  
Madam Chair, currently we are working with the Office of the Executive Secretary and the Office of the Cabinet Secretary.

**[01:18:44.300] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001266)  
And precisely for that?

**[01:18:45.740] DHSUD Undersecretary Eduardo P. Robles Jr.** (asr-001267)  
Yes, ma'am.

**[01:18:46.380] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001268)  
You have actually proposed that?

**[01:18:48.020] DHSUD Undersecretary Eduardo P. Robles Jr.** (asr-001269)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[01:18:48.720] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001270)  
So wala pang status yung request?

**[01:18:50.640] DHSUD Undersecretary Eduardo P. Robles Jr.** (asr-001271)  
So ngayon po, nagpapadala na po ng representative yung mga sa Office ng Executive Secretary, pati rin po sa Cabinet Secretary.

**[01:18:55.620] DHSUD Undersecretary Eduardo P. Robles Jr.** (asr-001272)  
Para po ma-finalize lang po.

**[01:18:57.300] DHSUD Undersecretary Eduardo P. Robles Jr.** (asr-001273)  
Gagawa na po ng isang kinatawag niyang proclamation na isasama na po yung buong listahan natin.

**[01:19:02.400] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001274)  
So parang consolidated proclamation?

**[01:19:04.600] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001275)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[01:19:05.020] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001276)  
And also, ma'am, the PCUP.

**[01:19:06.500] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001277)  
It's now under, directly, Office of the President.

**[01:19:10.040] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001278)  
Yes, under the Office of the President.

**[01:19:11.220] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001279)  
So one of the reasons po ito, kaya ginawang ganoon, is to...

**[01:19:13.780] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001280)  
And you do believe that it will really facilitate things?

**[01:19:16.600] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001281)  
Yes, yes.

**[01:19:17.660] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001282)  
And of course, you have to follow it up?

**[01:19:19.740] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001283)  
Yes, ma'am. We'll do that.

**[01:19:20.940] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001284)  
Okay.

**[01:19:21.740] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001285)  
Tama ho ba yan? Wala na po akong oras.

**[01:19:26.360] Committee Secretary** (asr-001286)  
One minute and 30 seconds. 30 seconds po.

**[01:19:30.593] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001286-split-1)

**[01:19:34.660] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001287)  
Thank you very much.

**[01:19:35.700] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001289)  
Lagi rin pong problema ng mga komunidad ang pagdagsa ng mga private complainants.

**[01:19:40.460] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001290)  
I'm sorry, private claimants na sa mga lupaing pinuklamaw na para sa kanilang dekade-dekade na ang nakalipas.

**[01:19:48.900] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001291)  
The delays in the distribution of titles and selas have allowed for these private claimants to emerge

**[01:19:55.820] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001292)  
and take advantage of the informal settler families.

**[01:20:00.900] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001293)  
Ang nangyayari tuloy, lagi nilang kinakaharap ang mga banta ng ebiksyon at demolisyon.

**[01:20:06.140] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001294)  
Now, ang tanong ko po is,

**[01:20:07.960] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001295)  
does DSUD or any of its key agencies provide legal assistance so the informal settler families have to fight for the land

**[01:20:16.300] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001296)  
that was already proclaimed for them decades ago in court and respond to other legal issues?

**[01:20:22.820] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001297)  
Lalo na yan, yung mga private claimants, bigla nalang mga sumusulpot po yan.

**[01:20:28.000] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001298)  
And the affected informal settler families become helpless in the face of these private claims.

**[01:20:38.120] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001299)  
Is there any mechanism?

**[01:20:40.180] DHSUD Undersecretary Marisol D.L. Anenias** (asr-001299-split-1)  
Currently po, there's no mechanism under the DSUD for the private complainants.

**[01:20:43.960] DHSUD Undersecretary Marisol D.L. Anenias** (asr-001300)  
Ang meron po ang DSUD are for those who complain against professional squatters and squatting syndicates

**[01:20:51.180] DHSUD Undersecretary Marisol D.L. Anenias** (asr-001301)  
at saka po yung mga anti-illegal real estate practices.

**[01:20:53.680] DHSUD Undersecretary Marisol D.L. Anenias** (asr-001302)  
But for the claimants of the land, ng mga privado po na complainants, wala po tayong mekanismo para doon.

**[01:20:59.120] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001303)  
Wala pa. And these are cases pending in court.

**[01:21:04.020] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001304)  
Yes?

**[01:21:04.540] DHSUD Undersecretary Marisol D.L. Anenias** (asr-001305)  
Yes.

**[01:21:05.520] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001306)  
And therefore, it's actually difficult for you.

**[01:21:07.920] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001307)  
To come up with that mechanism.

**[01:21:10.000] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001308)  
But mas madali siyempre yung going after the syndicates.

**[01:21:14.797] DHSUD Undersecretary Marisol D.L. Anenias** (asr-001309)  
Yes.

**[01:21:14.999] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001310)  
Of mga fake titles.

**[01:21:17.840] DHSUD Undersecretary Marisol D.L. Anenias** (asr-001311)  
Opo, kasi executive po sa police power po.

**[01:21:19.940] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001312)  
Okay.

**[01:21:20.740] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001313)  
Doon po sa expanded pambansang pabahay para sa Pilipino program or the 4PH.

**[01:21:25.980] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001314)  
Now the CMP, the Community Mortgage Program, which allows informal settler families to collectively own land,

**[01:21:34.540] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001315)  
has approved 36 projects covering 6,000.

**[01:21:37.740] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001316)  
Potential beneficiaries.

**[01:21:40.000] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001317)  
I'll just finish this line of questioning, Madam Chair.

**[01:21:43.000] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001319)  
How many of these projects are located in coastal areas for fisher folk communities?

**[01:21:49.880] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001320)  
SHFC President will reply.

**[01:21:52.200] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001321)  
Yan po yung sektor na medyo nakukulangan yung sa serbisyo.

**[01:21:59.460] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001322)  
Madam Chair, I will answer.

**[01:22:03.400] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001323)  
Let us check our data.

**[01:22:07.080] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001324)  
Madam Chair, so that we will identify those areas which were already approved along the shores.

**[01:22:17.520] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001325)  
So the coastal.

**[01:22:18.800] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001326)  
Right now, we have already approved 47, Madam.

**[01:22:22.020] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001327)  
And we have benefited almost 9,000 families.

**[01:22:27.100] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001328)  
But we will check based on our records.

**[01:22:29.860] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001329)  
And certainly there would be others.

**[01:22:32.020] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001330)  
There will be

**[01:22:32.440] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001331)  
Other coastal communities.

**[01:22:33.740] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001332)  
In fact, in our pipeline, there are already 60 applications.

**[01:22:37.780] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001333)  
That are pending with us.

**[01:22:39.940] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001334)  
Subject to availability of funds.

**[01:22:42.840] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001335)  
Okay.

**[01:22:43.360] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001336)  
My time is up.

**[01:22:44.320] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001337)  
So thank you very much.

**[01:22:45.620] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001338)  
Thank you so much.

**[01:22:46.980] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001339)  
Next to interpellate, Representative Lani Mercado-Revilla.

**[01:22:54.480] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001340)  
Magandang hapon po sa ating pong housing department.

**[01:22:58.380] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001341)  
Just a quick question, Madam Chair.

**[01:23:02.080] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001342)  
My time is up.

**[01:23:04.260] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001343)  
Bilis naman nun.

**[01:23:06.540] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001344)  
Anyway, my question is with the

**[01:23:09.860] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001345)  
NHA.

**[01:23:11.000] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001346)  
I have noticed that in the NEP, you have a budget of 370 million pesos for locally funded projects.

**[01:23:21.160] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001347)  
Resettlement program for ISFs affected by the Supreme Court, Mandamos, to clean up the Manila Bay.

**[01:23:28.600] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001348)  
Would this be budget enough?

**[01:23:31.020] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001349)  
I would like to ask how much a building would cost with 60 units?

**[01:23:38.340] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001350)  
Kasi alam ko ang 370 million.

**[01:23:41.720] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001351)  
Would be short for two buildings.

**[01:23:44.580] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001352)  
Am I correct, Madam Chair?

**[01:23:48.840] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-001353)  
Madam Chair, a building would cost around 70 to 90 million pesos.

**[01:23:53.720] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-001354)  
So this, I think, would be able to construct maybe around four buildings.

**[01:23:59.420] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001355)  
Four buildings.

**[01:24:02.000] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001356)  
I would speak first for my district.

**[01:24:04.680] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001357)  
There is a backlog of 2,000 plus units in our district.

**[01:24:11.720] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001358)  
I can't imagine how could NHA be able to answer to help our informal settler families

**[01:24:19.260] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001359)  
who's affected by the Supreme Court, Mandamos, to clean up Manila Bay

**[01:24:22.440] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001360)  
if NHA has only 370 million pesos at a budget of 70 million per building with 60 units.

**[01:24:32.940] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001361)  
So, Madam Chair, I think we should also consider

**[01:24:37.300] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001362)  
how many buildings can be constructed by NHA.

**[01:24:41.120] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001363)  
In a year's time.

**[01:24:42.780] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001364)  
And also, kamusta po yung pagta-transfer po natin ng mga informal settler families

**[01:24:49.440] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001365)  
in existing National Housing Authority projects?

**[01:24:56.040] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-001366)  
Tuloy-tuloy naman po ang pagta-transfer.

**[01:24:57.960] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-001367)  
But as of the moment, wala po amin na-transfer

**[01:24:59.920] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-001368)  
because mas kakaunti po yung napoproduce natin

**[01:25:03.560] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-001369)  
compared po sa dami ng kailangan pong i-transfer.

**[01:25:07.340] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-001370)  
And to add to all that,

**[01:25:08.660] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-001371)  
medyo na,

**[01:25:11.780] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-001372)  
medyo nahirapan

**[01:25:12.860] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-001373)  
lang kami sa projection

**[01:25:13.660] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-001374)  
because our projects are multi-year

**[01:25:15.300] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-001375)  
and based on the 3 gaas,

**[01:25:18.380] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-001376)  
we're getting more than 3 billion

**[01:25:19.980] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-001377)  
but naslash po yung NEP namin for this year.

**[01:25:23.040] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-001378)  
So, if hindi po ma-restore yan to previous levels as last year,

**[01:25:30.680] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-001379)  
matutuloy po namin yung mga ongoing

**[01:25:32.340] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-001380)  
but we're not able to accommodate po yung mga new projects

**[01:25:36.140] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001381)  
and to fund those new projects.

**[01:25:38.480] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001382)  
So, Madam Chair, I think there's a difficulty for NHA to,

**[01:25:42.540] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001383)  
to support the program of the President

**[01:25:45.220] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001384)  
when it comes to informal settler families,

**[01:25:47.680] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001385)  
especially to comply with the Manila Bay Mandamo.

**[01:25:49.920] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001386)  
So, I think we should support NHA on this.

**[01:25:53.640] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001387)  
Balik po tayo dun sa tanong kanina tungkol po sa EHAP.

**[01:25:59.000] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001388)  
Nasabi po nila na wala ng pondo para sa EHAP.

**[01:26:03.480] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001389)  
Marami pa hong mga local government units ang umaasa

**[01:26:07.520] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001390)  
sa pondong ito, like in my district, for example,

**[01:26:10.240] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001391)  
napangakuan po ang,

**[01:26:12.620] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001392)  
ilan sa mga pamilya doon

**[01:26:15.500] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001393)  
and you still have a shortage of around 117 families

**[01:26:20.720] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001394)  
who are, should be given 30,000 pesos

**[01:26:22.680] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001395)  
pero wala na po ang pondo.

**[01:26:24.520] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001396)  
So, how would NHA be able to comply with this shortage

**[01:26:31.060] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001397)  
and how can DBM assist them?

**[01:26:32.420] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001398)  
At marami pa hong mga buwan na natitira sa taong ito

**[01:26:35.780] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001399)  
para sa tulong na ito na kailangan ng ating mga mamamayan.

**[01:26:39.700] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001400)  
Regarding the funds for,

**[01:26:42.640] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001401)  
EHAP or IDSAB?

**[01:26:43.775] NHA General Manager Joeben A. Tai** (asr-001402)  
EHAP.

**[01:26:44.500] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001403)  
We were informed verbally by the Office of the President

**[01:26:50.140] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001404)  
that within the next two weeks,

**[01:26:51.620] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001405)  
we will be able to,

**[01:26:53.680] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-001406)  
they will be able to release the funds for this.

**[01:26:56.400] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001407)  
Looking forward to that, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:26:58.560] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001408)  
That is good news for us.

**[01:27:00.180] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001409)  
Thank you.

**[01:27:00.920] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001410)  
And also, there are

**[01:27:02.420] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001411)  
existing SHAPSI projects in my district

**[01:27:07.180] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001412)  
and I'm sure there are a lot in the different locations in the country.

**[01:27:11.020] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001413)  
Okay.

**[01:27:12.120] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001414)  
I hope SHAPSI would also consider

**[01:27:14.300] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001415)  
continuing SHAPSI projects

**[01:27:17.400] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001416)  
who don't have parking lots.

**[01:27:21.220] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001417)  
I speak specifically of Ciudad de Strike in our district.

**[01:27:25.080] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001418)  
There are,

**[01:27:25.620] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001419)  
nalipatan na po ito ng mga kababayan natin

**[01:27:28.940] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001420)  
from Las Piñas, Paranaque, Bacoor, and other areas.

**[01:27:33.720] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001421)  
But unfortunately, the community is in need of a parking lot building.

**[01:27:38.060] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001422)  
A lot has been given to the Homeowners Association

**[01:27:41.500] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001423)  
but unfortunately,

**[01:27:42.920] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001424)  
they cannot fund a parking lot building

**[01:27:44.980] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001425)  
because it would cost around 60 to 80 million pesos.

**[01:27:48.680] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001426)  
How can SHAPSI assist such kind of problems

**[01:27:52.460] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001427)  
in different communities?

**[01:27:54.329] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001428)  
Yes.

**[01:27:55.072] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001429)  
Madam Chair,

**[01:27:58.060] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001430)  
the Ciudad de Strike was built or constructed

**[01:28:01.300] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001431)  
prior to 2022.

**[01:28:05.660] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001432)  
But we can revisit the plans of Ciudad de Strike

**[01:28:09.600] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001433)  
and we'll see and study

**[01:28:11.460] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001434)  
how we can

**[01:28:12.780] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001435)  
accommodate that request

**[01:28:14.020] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001436)  
from the good congressman.

**[01:28:16.160] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001437)  
Yes.

**[01:28:16.840] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001438)  
It is a difficulty for the community

**[01:28:18.700] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001439)  
to make use and utilize the roads,

**[01:28:21.560] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001440)  
especially if there are emergencies.

**[01:28:23.860] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001441)  
Kung may sunog sa mga gusali

**[01:28:25.980] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001442)  
where they have around 60 units per building

**[01:28:29.380] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001443)  
and I think they have more than 40 buildings

**[01:28:33.700] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001444)  
already constructed phase 1 and phase 2.

**[01:28:36.260] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001445)  
So it is very detrimental

**[01:28:38.100] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001446)  
that a parking lot building be allocated.

**[01:28:41.780] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001447)  
And I hope,

**[01:28:42.280] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001448)  
SHAPSI,

**[01:28:42.940] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001449)  
should also consider

**[01:28:44.060] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001450)  
such developments

**[01:28:45.620] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001451)  
in your future projects,

**[01:28:47.820] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001452)  
be it township development, for that matter.

**[01:28:50.900] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001453)  
Madam Chair,

**[01:28:51.860] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001454)  
and as far as the projects

**[01:28:54.320] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001455)  
that were newly constructed,

**[01:28:56.360] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001456)  
we have adequate open space

**[01:28:58.400] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-001457)  
for these projects.

**[01:29:01.140] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001458)  
Thank you very much, Madam Chair.

**[01:29:03.020] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001459)  
That would be all.

**[01:29:04.100] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001460)  
And I hope,

**[01:29:04.800] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001461)  
and I second the motion

**[01:29:06.300] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001462)  
of our good chairperson of housing,

**[01:29:10.280] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001463)  
Congresswoman Copilar,

**[01:29:11.660] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001464)  
in adding,

**[01:29:12.800] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001465)  
more funding

**[01:29:14.300] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001466)  
for the Department of Housing

**[01:29:16.100] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001467)  
to support the housing programs

**[01:29:18.800] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001468)  
of the Bagong Pilipinas

**[01:29:20.640] Rep. Lani Mercado-Revilla** (asr-001469)  
and our President. Maraming salamat po.

**[01:29:21.600] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001470)  
Thank you, Representative Mercado.

**[01:29:24.860] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001471)  
We will just act on your motion

**[01:29:27.640] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001472)  
on the proper time.

**[01:29:30.440] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001473)  
Next to the pilot,

**[01:29:32.160] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001474)  
DML, Sergio Dagooc.

**[01:29:39.640] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001475)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:29:42.120] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001476)  
Magandang hapon po sa ating lahat.

**[01:29:44.380] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001477)  
Lalong-lalo na siya,

**[01:29:45.360] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001478)  
sa ating bisita

housing

**[01:30:58.440] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001488)

**[01:31:24.760] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001492)

**[01:54:21.660] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002071)  
-una po, nabanggit po kanina sa presentation natin

**[01:54:24.300] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002072)  
na meron po tayong na-streamline na na

**[01:54:26.300] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002073)  
-process. Ngayon bakit po ba halos pareho

**[01:54:28.880] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002074)  
ang proseso ng socialized housing sa

**[01:54:31.320] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002075)  
hindi socialized housing? Ang rason

**[01:54:33.220] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002076)  
po dyan kasi pareho pong buyer

**[01:54:34.520] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002077)  
yun. Pareho pong binibentaan, whether it's a

**[01:54:36.620] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002078)  
socialized housing project or an open market

**[01:54:38.980] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002079)  
project, we have to protect the homebuyer. So

**[01:54:42.120] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002080)  
ang ginawa po natin, in-streamline po natin ang proseso na apektado po pareho yung socialized housing and the

**[01:54:49.560] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002081)  
open market. So Yusek Henry will explain

**[01:54:52.180] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-002082)  
the streamlining po. Madam Chair, Mr. Congressman, we were able in June of this year, work out with ARTA the issuance of a Joint Memo Circular

**[01:55:04.820] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-002083)  
001-2026. Ang objective po nung document na iyon is

**[01:55:12.420] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-002084)  
to streamline the permitting. So if I may share what we have agreed with

**[01:55:16.880] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-002085)  
15 other government agencies. For the subdivision housing permit, yung requirements po from 223, ibababa po namin to 62 or a

**[01:55:29.600] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-002086)  
72% reduction in the requirements. As far

**[01:55:33.520] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-002087)  
as the condominium projects, 162 requirements will be brought down to 71 which is a

**[01:55:41.620] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-002088)  
56% reduction in the requirements. In terms of timetable po, different din po, we also agreed at

**[01:55:50.420] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-002089)  
three different stages of each of the processing, ibababa Abad din po namin ang requirements sa subdivision by as much as 76

**[01:56:00.160] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-002090)  
percent and then sa

**[01:56:02.060] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-002091)  
condominium as much as 75 percent. Once implemented,

**[01:56:06.740] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-002092)  
the total processing time will actually be reduced by 50 percent. Yun

**[01:56:12.300] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-002093)  
po ang target namin with

**[01:56:13.680] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-002094)  
the joint memo circular signed last June.

**[01:56:17.400] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002095)  
Okay, thank you

**[01:56:18.660] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002096)  
Madam Chair. So,

**[01:56:19.800] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002097)  
kailan yan siya ma-implement sir? More progressive po ito. We streamline continuously. Ang sinasabi lang natin, ang target natin, at the end of

**[01:56:31.900] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-002098)  
the process, we will be able to

**[01:56:33.320] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-002099)  
reduce the process by 50%. Hindi ba pwedeng yung sa

**[01:56:37.820] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-002100)  
socialized housing, mas streamlined

**[01:56:40.440] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002101)  
pa siya kaysa commercial housing?

**[01:56:42.180] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002102)  
Sabay-sabay. Commercial housing din

**[01:56:44.080] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002103)  
po kasi ang socialized. Binebenta rin po yun. Kaya

**[01:56:46.440] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002104)  
more or less pareho po ang requirement. Kaya ang sinistreamline natin, apektado po, bareho yung socialized housing and

**[01:56:52.460] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-002105)  
non

**[01:56:52.660] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-002106)  
-socialized housing projects. Okay. Ang point, Madam Chair, yung socialized housing at saka yung

**[01:57:02.460] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-002107)  
open market, medyo mas mababa yung margin para sa mga

**[01:57:08.300] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-002108)  
developers dun sa socialized housing. Ang point, Madam Chair, hindi ba pwedeng mas streamlined pa, hindi siya kapareho

**[01:57:16.560] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-002109)  
doon sa open market yung sa socialized housing? Pagkating

**[01:57:18.840] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-002110)  
po sa socialized housing, Madam Chair, mayroon po tayong reference, yung BP220, yung

**[01:57:24.120] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-002111)  
po ang reference ng socialized housing. Talaga

**[01:57:26.140] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-002112)  
pong mas mababa yung technical requirements ng socialized housing compared to the open market.

**[01:57:32.700] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-002113)  
Okay. Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:57:34.900] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-002114)

**[01:57:35.840] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-002115)

**[01:57:47.760] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-002116)

**[01:57:50.720] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-002117)

**[01:58:10.360] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-002120)

**[01:58:28.140] Rep. Elmer V. Catulpos** (asr-002123)

**[02:00:53.860] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-002159)

and also the

**[02:03:21.860] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-002188)

**[02:03:28.560] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-002189)

**[02:04:11.700] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002198)  
33,000 housing units.

**[02:05:13.000] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-002213)

**[02:05:23.600] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-002215)

**[02:05:29.680] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-002217)

**[02:05:32.720] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-002218)

**[02:06:10.620] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002227)

**[02:06:14.960] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002228)

**[02:06:24.780] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002230)  
Hindi pa rin naiwasan, masira ang mga tahanan, lalo ng mga pamilyang walang kakayahan magpatayo na mas matibay at disaster-resilient na bahay.

**[02:06:37.780] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002231)  
Kaya po, welcome the SUDE Integrated Disaster Shelter Assistance Program, or IDSAP,

**[02:06:45.000] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002232)  
which provides shelter assistance to families whose homes are damaged by the disaster.

**[02:06:51.160] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002233)  
However, the Commission on Audit found that the processing and distribution of

**[02:06:58.280] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002234)  
emergency shelter support, a component of IDSAP, were delayed by 68 to 343 days,

**[02:07:08.280] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002235)  
contrary to the objectives of the program of providing timely emergency shelter assistance.

**[02:07:16.620] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002236)  
May we ask the SUDE what caused this delay and what measures have been put in place to prevent them from recurring?

**[02:07:24.620] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002237)  
Madam Chair.

**[02:07:25.660] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002238)  
Madam Chair, if I may reply.

**[02:07:28.740] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002239)  
When I was appointed as Secretary, the IDSAP disbursement backlog was included in the backlog.

**[02:07:35.840] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002240)  
But we have already disbursed the available funds that will be disbursed.

**[02:07:42.860] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002241)  
So, the funds that we have not yet disbursed are the ones that are affected because the funds in the NHA have not yet been lowered.

**[02:07:49.960] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002242)  
So, the cash that we have disbursed has already been fully disbursed.

**[02:07:57.260] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002243)  
Kaya po, Madam Chair,

**[02:21:09.460] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002454)

**[02:21:11.620] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002455)  
Please help us.

**[02:21:12.560] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002456)

**[02:21:18.380] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002457)

**[02:21:21.720] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002458)

**[02:21:22.440] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002459)

**[02:21:23.520] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002460)

**[02:21:24.260] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-002461)  
Yes.

**[02:21:25.400] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-002462)

**[02:21:28.620] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-002463)

**[02:21:30.420] SHFC President and CEO Federico A. Laxa** (asr-002464)

**[02:21:39.520] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002466)

**[02:21:41.720] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002467)

**[02:21:43.480] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002468)  
Madam Chair.

**[02:21:44.020] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002469)  
Chair.

**[02:21:46.960] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-002470)  
Yes.

**[02:21:48.153] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002472)

**[02:21:58.040] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002475)

Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:23:01.040] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002501)

**[02:23:04.260] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002502)  
Okay.

**[02:23:04.960] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002503)

**[02:23:06.940] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002504)

**[02:23:12.180] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002505)

**[02:23:14.600] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002506)

**[02:23:17.760] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002507)

**[02:23:25.680] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002508)

**[02:23:30.580] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002509)  
I support the budget.

**[02:23:32.180] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002510)  
That's what I've said.

**[02:23:33.880] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002511)  
And you know, Ilocos region is the next.

**[02:23:36.980] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002512)

**[02:23:41.560] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002513)  
So sana, bisan at par na lang maging 45, 55, no?

**[02:23:49.140] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002514)  
Kawawa naman our region.

**[02:23:51.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002515)  
So I hope you'll also be able to give our region, especially the second district of

**[02:23:55.940] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002516)  
which is mine, or the province of Negros Oriental, a thought on how to serve better our constituents

**[02:24:02.440] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002517)  
on the housing issues.

**[02:24:05.180] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002518)  
Anyway, aside from that, do we have any intervention when houses of indigent constituents

**[02:24:10.040] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002519)  
are totally damaged by fire?

**[02:24:13.040] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002520)  
How do we go about it?

**[02:24:15.380] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002521)  
And because you know why, before their houses are being consumed by the fire, they don't

**[02:24:21.960] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002522)  
do any rental or whatever.

**[02:24:23.840] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002523)  
And suddenly, when there's a fire that consumes their houses, they, of course, there's nowhere

**[02:24:28.600] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002524)  
to go but to do the, they will be renting.

**[02:24:32.380] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002525)  
So I hope we will be able to do something that, parang maka, they will be able to build

**[02:24:40.300] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002526)  
even just a modest house.

**[02:24:43.500] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002527)  
Thank you.

**[02:24:54.460] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002532)

**[02:24:56.940] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002533)

**[02:24:59.640] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002534)

**[02:25:00.860] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002535)

**[02:25:04.740] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002536)

**[02:25:09.100] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002537)

**[02:25:14.160] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002538)

**[02:25:19.700] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002539)

**[02:25:30.880] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002541)

**[02:25:37.360] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002542)

**[02:25:45.720] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002543)

**[02:25:49.660] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002544)  
Thank you so much.

**[02:25:53.500] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002546)  
Thank you.

**[02:25:54.700] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-002547)  
Thank you so much, Secretary.

**[02:26:25.760] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002551)  
Magandang hapon po sa inyong lahat.

**[02:26:29.860] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002552)  
Marami sana akong tanong pero hiyo ako na masyadong magtanong dahil mukhang buong araw na kayong nabubugbog dito.

**[02:26:37.000] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002553)  
At bilang dating kasama din po sa pamilya ng mga KSA,

**[02:26:41.360] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002554)

**[02:26:44.460] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002555)

**[02:26:52.600] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002556)  
So, naipangako namin sa mga tao at it turns out na wala palang pondo.

**[02:26:59.360] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002557)  
So, sisiguraduhin namin magiging bahagi kami na mapondohan nito.

**[02:27:03.540] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002558)  
At sana'y yung aming mga nakapila na ho dyan sa mariki ng mga nasunugan,

**[02:27:09.320] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002559)  
ay kukunti lang naman yung sekretary, GM eh, yung sa amin.

**[02:27:13.880] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002560)

**[02:27:20.580] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002561)  
Because, really, when you look at the DBM, there's major underspending.

**[02:27:25.200] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002562)  
So, I checked when we had the first meeting with the DBM, they said that there's no underspending,

**[02:27:30.540] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002563)

**[02:27:36.060] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002564)  
And, you know, housing, as I always say, is the biggest multiplier talaga.

**[02:27:40.500] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002565)  
Ayun, paulit-ulit tayo dyan sa mga ginagawa natin.

**[02:27:43.680] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002566)  
Yan ang natutunan ko sa pag-ibig. Natutuwa ko.

**[02:27:46.540] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002567)  
Madam Chair, itong si CEO Malin Acosta, nung nasa pag-ibig ako, siya ang aming manager ng San Fernando branch.

**[02:27:58.180] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002568)  
Itim na itim pa ang buhok lahat niyan, ano ha? Ngayon, parang wala na natira, ha?

**[02:28:04.000] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002569)  
So, I'm happy.

**[02:28:09.000] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002570)

**[02:28:16.500] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002571)  
So, nakakatuwa po.

**[02:28:18.400] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002572)  
Pero, panguli ko na lang, itong ating CMP program sa ilalim ng Social Housing Finance Corporation, hindi ako sanay magbigay ng papuri eh.

**[02:28:26.840] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002573)  
Kasi, pero talagang si GM Rico ay, ah, President pala.

**[02:28:35.460] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002574)  
Nasanay kasi akong GM eh, nung nasa NHA.

**[02:28:38.360] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002575)  
No, but genuinely, I think Rico represents a bureaucrat who is able to find solutions.

**[02:28:45.680] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002576)  
Wala, wala. Walang pag may problema, parating may sagot.

**[02:28:49.360] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002577)  
Imbis na balakid, o itong problema, etc. lahat.

**[02:28:53.660] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002578)  
Kulang na lang pag sinabi ko, ito, wala ka bang ibabahay? Wala na eh. Ah, hindi, bahay pa rin natin.

**[02:28:59.360] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002579)  
Hindi naman, nagbibiro ako. But, even in terms of...

**[02:29:04.580] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002580)  
Ah, kasi maraming kabit-kabit po eh, na usapin, na related din po sa HUDC, or sorry, sa DSUD.

**[02:29:12.320] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002581)  
So, nagpapasalamat ako kay CEO Laksa na wala kaming nilapit sa kanya at lahat po yan ay mga CMP.

**[02:29:19.700] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002582)  
So, wala pong kalokohan dyan, walang pinagkakakitaan, kung ano man po yan.

**[02:29:23.440] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002583)  
Yan po ay talagang serbisyo lang na matagal nang inaasam-asam ng ating mga kababayan.

**[02:29:31.080] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002584)  
So, I hope, I'm not a senior member of the Committee on Appropriation, but I'll be the first one to say,

**[02:29:37.900] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002585)  
ang campaign na magkaroon ng pondo talaga ang ating CMP program, dahil ito talaga ang pinaka-emancipating program sa buong DSUD.

**[02:29:48.040] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002586)  
So, salamat po sa lahat ng suporta.

**[02:29:51.780] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002587)  
At saka yung marami pa po kami re-rehistro ng mga ho-wa.

**[02:29:56.100] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002588)  
Ganyan lang naman po, mababaw lang po ang aming mga pangailangan.

**[02:29:59.580] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002589)  
Pero ang sinisigurado po namin ay we want to support.

**[02:30:03.420] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002590)  
We encourage them to register, to regularize, so that they are protected also under the law.

**[02:30:09.900] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002591)  
At mabait po yung inyong regional director, nakausap ko rin po, na inaasikaso.

**[02:30:15.520] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002592)  
Asan ba yung ating regional director na NCR?

**[02:30:18.040] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002593)  
Ah, wala dito? Ah, baka bising-bising tatrabawan, no?

**[02:30:21.940] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002594)  
So, pag iniimbita po namin siya, sekretary, para pumunta po sa city mismo, para ipaliwanag, ano yung mga requirements.

**[02:30:27.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002595)  
Kasi marami sa ating mga kababayan talaga niloloko eh.

**[02:30:32.260] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002596)  
Bising-bising po, may event sa Marikina raw sa Thursday.

**[02:30:35.280] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002597)  
Ah, yun. Yung pinaka-importante. Importante, imbitado ako dyan, sekretary, diba?

**[02:30:41.460] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002598)  
Madalas kasi di na ako na-iimbita eh, pero parang this time na-iimbita ako. Maraming salamat po ulit dyan.

**[02:30:46.500] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002599)  
No, but seriously, many LGUs...

**[02:30:48.000] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002600)  
Use the weakness of our homeowners association, especially communities, to pry on the instability of their ownership.

**[02:30:58.600] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002601)  
So, they're always at the beck and call of City Hall.

**[02:31:01.980] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002602)  
At sila pa mismo nagko-complicate ng mga...

**[02:31:05.300] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002603)

**[02:31:10.680] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002604)

**[02:31:13.640] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002605)  
Pero pag bumababa po, at ang nagsasabi po, ako na po nagpapaliwanag.

**[02:31:18.140] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002606)  
Sinasabi ko, ito nasa Magna Carta, ito nasa batas.

**[02:31:20.820] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002607)

**[02:31:26.340] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002608)  
Pero hindi silang maniwala pag sinabihan ng munisipyo ng iba.

**[02:31:30.080] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002609)  
Pero pag ang nagsalita na po, ay taga-dissued, ito lang po ang kailangan ninyo para kayo po ay ma-recognize.

**[02:31:37.400] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002610)  
Ito lang po, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5.

**[02:31:39.200] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002611)

**[02:31:45.600] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002612)

**[02:31:51.000] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-002613)

**[03:00:56.560] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002998)

**[03:01:00.920] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-002999)

**[03:01:04.040] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003000)

**[03:01:12.580] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003001)

**[03:01:16.680] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003002)

**[03:01:29.280] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003003)

**[03:01:31.340] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003004)

**[03:01:34.840] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003005)

**[03:01:39.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003006)  
saan po sila, saan po yung marirekomenda natin na pinakamalapit sa kanila na maaaring malipatan?

**[03:02:00.680] DHSUD Secretary Jose Ramon P. Aliling** (asr-003010)

**[03:02:07.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003011)

**[03:02:08.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003012)

**[03:48:56.700] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003167)

**[03:49:07.540] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003168)  
-100 tayo before the end of the year.

**[03:49:10.740] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003169)  
Madam Chair, the reason for the low utilization sa budget last year was because there was a change of plan. Si

**[03:49:18.940] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003170)  
Secretary came in in May of last year.

**[03:49:21.560] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003171)  
At that time, yung

**[03:49:22.640] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003172)  
expanded 4PH natin

**[03:49:24.040] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003173)  
was a single modality which is the 4 vertical.

**[03:49:28.000] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003174)  
So there was a change in June-July of last year that was expanded to now cover a number of modalities.

**[03:49:35.380] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003175)  
And not only limited to vertical but horizontal, kasama na rin natin yung mga

**[03:49:40.840] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003176)  
rental, yung transitory,

**[03:49:43.480] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003177)  
and then we also have the proclamations and the CMPs.

**[03:49:47.620] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003178)  
So sinama na po natin yan.

**[03:49:49.600] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003179)  
And that's the reason why towards the end of last year

**[03:49:52.520] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003180)  
and early this year, marami na po ang nabibigyan

**[03:49:55.840] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003181)  
and that is the reason also why tumataas na rin yung utilization natin.

**[03:50:00.680] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003182)  
The other thing is because there was, last year, prior to the end of the year, there was a clamor for a change in the price ceiling.

**[03:50:09.780] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003183)  
So many of our developers were not constructing because they felt lugi po sila kung magtatayo sila.

**[03:50:15.420] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003184)  
So in December of last year, the department together with DepDev approved the adjustment in price ceiling.

**[03:50:23.540] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003185)  
So this will allow more developers to come in and participate in the development of horizontal and vertical housing.

**[03:50:30.220] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003186)  
So that's what we've also seen.

**[03:50:32.180] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003187)  
there is now a influx of developers constructing to be able to address those units.

**[03:50:40.270] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003188)  
Now, one of the other items that we were not able to do last year was yung ISSP. Yun yung

**[03:50:49.060] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003189)  
hindi po namin nagamit

**[03:50:50.860] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003190)  
but we've reduced actually

**[03:50:52.820] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003191)  
this year's requirement.

**[03:50:54.100] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003192)  
So, hindi na ganoon kasing laki po yung utilization or yung funding requirement namin for the ISSP namin. Opo, smaller na po yung

**[03:51:04.580] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003193)  
ISSP namin this year.

**[03:51:07.000] DHSUD Senior Undersecretary Henry L. Yap** (asr-003194)  
So, a big portion of it really was the change in the policy from a single modality to a multiple modality program.

**[03:51:18.620] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003195)

before the end of the year.
